**How it works now** (at `f649193`)
- **Membership.** A leak is decided on the device's membership, read in the same provisioning hold as the rules: LoRa `"0x%08lX"` through `parse_hex_id()`, BLE by `strcasecmp` on the MAC string, the valve by a non-empty `valve_mac`. A sensor removed between the gate and the decision, a neighbour's sensor passed as UNKNOWN while provisioning was busy (N4), and a kept report replayed after its sensor's removal (15o residual 1, sensor half) are ignored with W `Leak from %s sensor %s ignored - not in this hub's device list` (not rate-limited; reachable only between a removal and its apply). A dry report still untracks its source whatever the device set. The busy fallback (the last applied set) is unchanged.
- **Kept reports** (a rules lock busy 1 s or more): per source at most one wet and one dry, alternating; a wet report replaces a kept dry one, its own source's first, then the newest sensor dry, the valve's dry last (the valve never re-sends). A wet report is lost (E line) only when 4 distinct sources' wet reports are kept. A displaced source stays wet in the engine (fail-safe: auto-clear waits, `leak_reset` is refused, until its next report). Host fuzz, 300k sequences against `fdb8c28`: no missed latch; every lost wet has 4 distinct wet sources kept.
- **The owed valve-swap purge** runs exactly once, first, in the next hold of the rules lock on any task, before anything reads the active-leak set. In the window before it runs, the C2D paths fail closed. It can run `incident_save_to_nvs()` (an NVS commit) in the hold that evaluates a leak: flash latency on that one leak → CLOSE, only while a purge is owed (a valve swap during a 1 s busy hold).
- **Twin and lifecycle** read one locked summary; a busy read builds nothing (W `Twin report not built - provisioning busy for 1 s, built again later`, `Lifecycle not built - …`, at most one a minute), the report stays owed with the 5 s back-off, and a pass waits at most 1 s (the summary fails at once for 2 s after a timeout). The wire order cmd_ack, twin, snapshot is kept; a provision's snapshot waits for its back-off-delayed twin once.
- **MQTT stop** (on `wifi_task`): a refusal (`ESP_FAIL` with `run` false: a client whose task has not yet run its first line) is retried 10 × 20 ms; every successful stop then settles 20 ms. A never-started client costs about 200 ms and esp-mqtt's `Client asked to stop, but was not started` 10 times per stop.
- **Snapshot:** realistic full hub (16 BLE + 16 LoRa + valve) about 7,475 B; worst case (31-character fields of quotes) 9,575 B; control characters now print as spaces (13,915 B before, refused for ever); worst event 491 B, within the offline buffer's 512 B entry. The cJSON tree of a full hub is about 27 KB plus the 8.6 KB print block: never benched.

**Log lines** (new or changed; none prints a credential, and the production tool's patterns match none):
- `RULES_ENGINE`: W `Leak from %s sensor %s ignored - not in this hub's device list`; W `Rules lock busy for 1 s - %s report from %s sensor %s kept, …` (now also `dry`); W `… the wet one from %s sensor %s replaces its last kept dry one`; W `… the dry one from %s sensor %s gives way to the wet one from %s sensor %s`; `Valve replaced: …` can now print from the esp-mqtt task (a C2D command ran the owed purge).
- `IOTHUB`: W `Twin report not built - provisioning busy for 1 s, built again later`; W `MQTT stop refused %d time(s) - the client had just started`; W `MQTT stop refused %d times (%s) - taken as stopped, no client task seen`. `MQTT client stopped on wifi_task in N.N s` now includes 0.02 s more.
- `TELEMETRY_V2`: W `Lifecycle not built - provisioning busy for 1 s, built again later`; W `Snapshot is %u B, over the %u B the MQTT outbox is sized for …`; E `Snapshot is %u B, over the %u B MQTT outbox limit - it will be refused`; E `Snapshot not built - out of memory` now also on a print failure.
- `override_enable` can answer `NO_INCIDENT` with the existing W `override_enable: no active incident to override` while the hub's own RMLEAK clear is in flight after an auto-clear or `leak_reset`.

**Memory** (objects at `-Og`, the project's flags, `545b8f2` against `f649193`, the same regenerated `sdkconfig.h`; the integrator's figures match the fixer's):

| Object | Code (`.text` + `.literal`) | `.rodata` | `.bss` |
|---|---|---|---|
| `rules_engine.c` | +1,113 B | +334 B | +1 B |
| `provisioning_manager.c` | +580 B | 0 | +9 B |
| `app_iothub.c` | +597 B | +242 B | +5 B |

- `.data` 0. Newly linked: `cJSON_PrintBuffered` and `cJSON_PrintPreallocated` (about 0.2 KB of flash; discarded in CP5's map). The 1-byte and pointer atomics compile inline (S32C1I loops), with no libatomic call. No IRAM: DIRAM `.text` stays 113,387 B.
- **The `sdkconfig` lines themselves** (the integrator, the affected IDF objects compiled both ways, 15t): `.bss` **−2,008 B** (NimBLE's `ble_adv_reattempt`, `ble_conn_reattempt`, `reattempt_conn`), code −1,491 B, `.rodata` −287 B. The plan's table said about −40 B of `.bss`.
- **Stack:** `build_twin_reported` 416 → 64 B and `telemetry_v2_build_lifecycle` 416 → 64 B (off `iothub_task`'s deepest path); `build_snapshot` 1,776 → 1,792 B; `build_envelope` 64 → 96 B; new `valve_purge_if_owed` 32 B + `valve_replaced_locked` 48 B, now also on the esp-mqtt task (6,144 B; not measured).

**The council** (rtos, leak safety, cloud and build; on `fa05390` … `f649193`): **3/3 SHIP, no blocking issue.** Checked: the only nested acquisition is still rules → provisioning, and provisioning never calls back; one writer per cross-task variable; the owed purge runs before every reader of the active-leak set, exactly once (fetch-or / exchange); every eviction branch is bounded and fails safe; RMLEAK-before-CLOSE (`rules_engine.c` 989-1061) untouched; the stop retry against IDF 5.5.1 `mqtt_client.c` 1639-1948 (`send_disconnect_msg()` returns `ESP_OK` even on a failed write, so R18's own case is unreachable; the reachable one is `run` still false); the outbox comparison (`mqtt_client.c:2181`); the regeneration recipe run through kconfgen 2.5.0 in scratch (the deprecated block's `CONFIG_ESP32_WIFI_*=32` aliases do not override the new 16); TLS: `esp_mbedtls_write()` splits at `OUT_CONTENT_LEN`, nothing reads the peer certificate after the handshake, no client certificate. The two review rounds before the council found 20 issues (14 fixed, 6 recorded below). Of the review's 10 findings before the vote (RTOS-WP3-1 to -3, WP3S-1, F1-F6), nine are fixed or documented (`f639297` … `f649193`; F4 as a corrected bench note, F5 as a comment), and F1, sent to the portal stream, does not hold (15t finding 1). The fixer's three review rounds of its own fixes found 8, 8 and 1 issues; 7 were fixed.

**Provisional constants for the bench** (each one named constant with a comment):
- `CONFIG_ESP_WIFI_DYNAMIC_RX_BUFFER_NUM` / `TX` 16 (plan §8's G3 contingency may lower RX to 12: change the defaults line and the guard together); `CONFIG_DEFAULT_AP_CHANNEL` 11 (D7; G0 records the fallback AP's channel); `CONFIG_MBEDTLS_SSL_OUT_CONTENT_LEN` 2048.
- `MQTT_TX_MAX_MESSAGE` 10,240 and `MQTT_OUTBOX_BACKLOG_BYTES` 2,048 (`app_iothub.h`): from the host model's 9,575 B worst case; a full hub's real snapshot has never been measured.
- `SNAP_PRINT_TYPICAL_BYTES` 230, `SNAP_PRINT_BASE_BYTES` 1,024, `SNAP_PRINT_DEVICE_BYTES` 280 (`telemetry_v2.c`).
- `MQTT_STOP_TRIES` 10, `MQTT_STOP_RETRY_MS` 20 (also the settle; at 100 Hz that is 2 ticks, 10-20 ms).
- `SUMMARY_BUSY_HOLDOFF_MS` 2,000; `PROV_BUSY_LOG_S` 60; `TWIN_REPORT_RETRY_MS` 5,000 (now also after a busy build); the F3 snapshot hold, once per owed report, at most 5 s plus one 2 s poll.

**For the user's decision** (unchanged by WP3, still open)
1. **15p W1, the QoS 0 twin report** with IoT Hub's 204 as the acknowledgement: the council recommended it for WP3; not built without the user's decision.
2. **D4's leak-path reordering:** lands only if CP7 measures a leak → CLOSE over 200 ms (15k item 12's method).
3. **`network.timeout_ms`** (15m): no decision, unchanged.
4. **15i residual 6:** `esp_mqtt_client_start()`'s result is still not checked (`ESP_FAIL` means "no memory" or "already running", which need opposite `g_mqtt_running` values); a never-started client now costs about 200 ms at each stop.

**For the app and cloud teams** (through the user)
- A label or hub name containing control characters shows them as spaces in telemetry; the twin's reported `hub_name` keeps the exact value.
- While provisioning is busy, the twin report and the lifecycle arrive up to about 5 s later instead of carrying defaults.
- `override_enable` can answer `NO_INCIDENT` right after an auto-clear or a `leak_reset`, while the hub's RMLEAK clear is still in flight (a refusal: it fails safe).

**Residual risks** (none blocks CP7; owner in brackets)
1. **The valve half of 15o residual 1** (pre-existing): the valve's leak source has no MAC, so after a swap whose device-set apply is deferred longer than the new valve takes to link, the new valve's wet report can be purged as the old valve's. The new valve still got RMLEAK and CLOSE (fail closed), but the incident is released, so `leak_reset` is then accepted while its probe is wet. [a MAC-tagged valve source: 2.1.5 or WP8]
2. **5 wet sources, the 5th the valve,** inside one busy hold: the valve's wet report is lost (E line) and, as it never re-sends, the 10 s all-clear can clear RMLEAK when the 4 sensors dry while the probe is wet; the valve's own interlock still protects. [none; needs 5 sources wet at once plus a 1 s hold]
3. **A displaced sensor's dry report** leaves it wet in the engine until its next report (BLE heartbeat 5 min, LoRa next packet): auto-clear waits and `leak_reset` is refused meanwhile. Fail-closed. [none]
4. **The owed purge's NVS commit** can add flash latency to one leak → CLOSE while a purge is owed. [CP7 item: D4's 200 ms]
5. **An owed `g_rmleak_clear_owed` that never lands** while the valve keeps reading RMLEAK=1: `override_enable` answers `NO_INCIDENT` until a `leak_reset` sends the clear again. Blocks water, not protection. [the app team]
6. **The stale `STOPPED_BIT` race** of IDF 5.5.1 esp-mqtt (a few instructions wide, pre-existing) is covered only by the 20 ms settle, a timing patch; a `mqtt_task` starved longer can still overlap its teardown with a SAS `set_config` (15m residual 3, narrower). [none in the app]
7. **15o's register items (1)-(7)** (the valve identity when provisioning is busy, `on_valve_connected`'s timeout, the 1 s timeout in `take_pending_telemetry`, a full `ble_cmd_queue`, `rules_config` merging into defaults on a timeout, and two delays) are not fixed. [a later WP]
8. **The full-hub snapshot's memory** (a 27 KB cJSON tree plus an 8.6 KB block) is unbenched; if it fails to build, the fix is a streaming builder. [CP7: a full hub if one can be assembled]
9. **Labels and hub names are sanitised at output only;** `sensor_meta.c` / `hub_identity.c` could reject control characters at intake. [optional]
10. **Not taken (altitude):** one coalesced kept record per source instead of the 4-slot list with eviction rules. [a later release]

**Closed by WP3 core:** 15o "For the user's decision" items 1 and 2 (decided 2026-10-02, done in `1afb368`), 15o residuals 2, 3 and 6, 15o's sensor half of residual 1 and N4's gate-UNKNOWN check, 15p W2 and W3, 15n R18 and 15m residual 3 (R18's DISCONNECT-write case is unreachable in IDF 5.5.1), 15i residual 5 (sanitising at output, and the W/E lines).

**What to watch at CP7** (15t has the order)
- **The build:** with today's `sdkconfig` the six guards stop it, by design; the regeneration first (15t). TLS with `OUT_CONTENT_LEN` 2048, no kept peer certificate and the dynamic buffer is a combination no IDF 5.5.1 CI config runs: check that DPS and MQTT connect, a SAS-renewal reconnect, a reconnect beside the SoftAP, and that a full snapshot publishes; watch for `esp-tls … write error`, mbedTLS −0x7200 / −0x7780 or a failed handshake. The idma largest-block dip during a TLS write should be about 2.4 KB instead of about 4.4 KB.
- **The leak path (P11, P14, the smoke subset):** RMLEAK then CLOSE unchanged; record leak → CLOSE against D4's 200 ms. A W `Leak from %s sensor %s ignored - not in this hub's device list` for a sensor provisioned on the bench hub is a release blocker.
- **The kept-report and owed-purge paths** run only with a rules lock held 1 s or more (CP6 item 17's hold image): flap the valve probe 4 or more times inside the hold, then wet a sensor: expect the latch, the close and no E `… is lost`; a valve swap during a hold prints `Valve replaced: …` once (from esp-mqtt if a C2D command lands first), and the new dry valve is not closed at its first link.
- **MQTT stops** at link loss, SoftAP start and SAS renewal: `MQTT client stopped on wifi_task in N.N s`; a lone esp-mqtt `Client asked to stop, but was not started` followed by `MQTT stop refused 1 time(s) - the client had just started` is expected; `… taken as stopped, no client task seen` followed later by esp-mqtt's `Client has started` is a finding.
- **A full hub, if one can be assembled:** a snapshot of about 7.5 KB, no `Snapshot is …` W/E line, no `Snapshot not built - out of memory`; record the idma minimum at each build.

**Docs still to follow (WP10 register).** `505eddc`'s message gives `provisioning_manager.o` as +12 B (+234 B in that commit, +456 B cumulative; `361ca55`'s message corrects it). HANDOFF 15k's "Report at once" list (around line 1375) treats esp-mqtt's `Client asked to stop, but was not started` as a finding: since `e315eda` it is expected once before `MQTT stop refused N time(s) - the client had just started`, and 10 times before `… taken as stopped`. 15j and the untracked `CP6_FULL_TEST_PLAN.md` B5 ("`sdkconfig` unchanged") hold for CP6 only. `MANUAL_TEST_PLAN.md` has no WP3 step.

### 15r. WP4: the setup portal's intake and the phone UX (C6, C7, C8, C9, C10b, C12, C13; D9 kept) (2026-10-06)

**Why.** Plan §11's WP4 (§6.2, §6.4, §7, §11): the portal's httpd bounds (C6), the gzipped page (C7), one owner for every connect, with a Connect committed only at its IP (C8), the page rebuilt around `status.json` (C9), the network list served from a cache (C10b), Finish (C12), the SoftAP's channel-switch announcement and the scan dwell (C13), and §6.4's deletions in the app. **D9: Forget/Disconnect stays** (`DELETE /connect.json` and the page's Disconnect button are kept); D10: only the setup page calls this API, so it may change. The channel-11 line itself landed with WP3's `fa05390`; WP4 set the component's Kconfig default to 11 to match. The stream owned `components/wifi_portal`, `main/app_wifi/app_wifi.c` and `main/wifi_reset/reset_button.c`.

**Commits** on `fix/2.1.4`: 15 implementer commits (`146498c` … `6c82af4`: 7 packages and 8 fixes of its own three review rounds) and 29 fixer commits (`3392935` … `9ba8e9c`), each compiled alone in the scratchpad with the project's flags, no warning, no IRAM, DRAM or RTC section. Nothing pushed, amended or rebased; line endings kept (the C sources and `index.html` CRLF in the worktree, stored LF).

**API now** (only the setup page uses it, D10): `GET /` and the assets gzipped (`HEAD` headers only; 406 only for `Accept-Encoding: identity` alone or an explicit gzip refusal); `GET /ap.json` from the cache (`?bg=1` for background polls; 503 when the heap cannot hold the copy); `POST /scan.json` (Rescan; the answer says when the next scan may run); `GET /status.json` (adds `reason`, with the new code 250 = no IP, and `pend`, the candidate's SSID); `POST /connect.json` (pct or raw credentials, optional channel hint; 400 with a JSON reason, 503 on a full queue); `DELETE /connect.json` (kept, D9); `POST /finish.json` (409 when not connected). Every answer carries `Cache-Control: no-store`; everything is refused off the SoftAP's subnet (the LAN is closed at accept).

**Log lines** (none prints a password; `status.json` never carries one; the production tool matches none):
- Activity names in `APP_WIFI: portal client <IP>: first %s, …`: `user request (list, Rescan, Connect, Disconnect or Finish)` replaces `Connect/Disconnect request`, `background poll (list)` replaces `network list request`; `DNS query`, `captive probe (302 sent)`, `page request` and `status request` unchanged.
- New `APP_WIFI` I: `Wi-Fi setup page: Connect - attempt started`; `Wi-Fi setup page: Connect - no attempt (the network in use, refused or replaced)`; `Wi-Fi setup page: Finish - the SoftAP stops N.N s after the IP`; `SoftAP tail: Finish on the setup page - it stops N.N s after the IP`.
- `APP_WIFI: Wi-Fi channel at AP start: radio %u (SoftAP configured %u), …` now prints `DEFAULT_AP_CHANNEL` (11), the channel the driver's SoftAP is configured with (15t, interaction finding 1); it printed the NVS settings blob's value before (1 on hubs set up before 2.1.4), which the driver never used.
- New `wifi_manager` W/E (printed under `main.c`'s WARN cap): `ORDER_DISCONNECT_STA: the STA is not connected - the saved network is erased now`; `user connect: …` (several, among them `user connect: the candidate got its IP - it is the network in use now, saved`, at W since `a5e5ae2`); `no STA_DISCONNECTED 3000 ms after esp_wifi_disconnect() - …`; `a late STA_DISCONNECTED of an attempt already ended - ignored`; `an IP of an attempt already counted as ended - not saved, left`; `an IP on a network other than the one in use, not a Connect's - not saved`; `the network in use is not saved (…) - tried again at the next IP…`; `an IP while a forget is under way - nothing saved`; `forget: the zeroed network not saved (…) - its keys erased instead`; E `forget: not saved (…), not erased (…) - a reboot may rejoin the network forgotten`; `retry: queue full …`, `AP stop: queue full …`, `restore: queue full …`; `esp_wifi_set_scan_parameters failed (…)`.
- New `http_server` W: `session closed: internal DMA free N B (needs 12288) - N since the server start` (at most one per 10 s) and `POST connect.json refused (400): …`. Each refused session also makes httpd print its own `W httpd_txrx: httpd_sock_err: error in recv : 128` (that tag is not capped): expected for a LAN connection during the tail and for each low-heap refusal.
- Removed: `APP_WIFI` W `Wi-Fi forget with the STA not connected - wifi_manager erases the saved network` and its E line.

| `http_app.c` | +1,963 B | +379 B | +12 B | 0 |

- **The page in flash:** 57,399 B embedded at CP5 (`style.css` 16,725, `code.js` 18,208, `index.html` 15,430, the PNG 7,036, each with its length word) → 20,204 B (`index.html.gz` 3,712, `code.js.gz` 5,678, `style.css.gz` 3,766, the PNG 7,032, plus the length words): **−37.2 KB**. Each text asset fits one 5,760 B TCP send buffer; `code.js` has 82 B to spare (its headers make the first send one more ACK round trip).

Both canary-checked: an overflow reboots.

**The council** (robustness, phones, build; on `146498c` … `9ba8e9c`): **3/3 SHIP, no blocking issue.** Checked: no new reboot path (no `ESP_ERROR_CHECK`, assert or abort in the new runtime code; every post bounded); percent decoding never reads past the terminator, NUL refused, lengths after decoding, a 64-character password must be hex; every reply is valid JSON (the worst `status.json` is exactly 510 B, with a `{}` fallback); the 10 s reset still works (it erases NVS directly under the NVS mutex); D9 kept; the C8 state machine traced through replace, wait, abort, forget in every state, a replaced candidate's late IP and an IP during a forget; `gz_asset.py` reproduced with IDF's Python and the 46 headless-Chrome scenarios run against the stripped `code.js` (the bytes the build embeds): 46/46; ES5 plus `fetch`/`Promise` only, so iOS 10.3+ WKWebView and Android WebView; the CMake chain against IDF 5.5.1 (the requirements pass returns at `idf_component_register`; the `.gz` outputs and `target_add_binary_data` in one directory; `ninja clean` removes them).

**Provisional constants for the bench** (one named constant each, with a comment):
- `WIFI_MANAGER_AP_CSA_COUNT` 3: G0 / P-8 read the driver's `csa_count` line and decide 3 or 5.
- `HTTP_APP_MAX_OPEN_SOCKETS` 5: G-CNA S1 picks 4, 5 or 7; G1 re-checks page p90.
- `HTTP_APP_SESSION_MIN_FREE` 12 KB of internal DMA-capable heap: G3's E2 laptop replay; any `session closed: internal DMA free …` line in a single-phone setup means the gate is mis-set.
- `WIFI_MANAGER_SCAN_ACTIVE_MAX_MS` 60 and `WIFI_MANAGER_SCAN_HOME_DWELL_MS` 100 (every station scan, connect and rejoin scans included): G7.
- `WIFI_MANAGER_USER_WAIT_MS` 8,000 (plan C8); `WIFI_MANAGER_USER_ATTEMPT_MS` 25,000, `WIFI_MANAGER_ABORT_WAIT_MS` 3,000, `WIFI_MANAGER_STALE_LEAVE_MS` 5,000, `WIFI_MANAGER_SCAN_RETRY_MS` 10,000, `WIFI_MANAGER_LIST_LOCK_MS` 1,000 and the list-allocation retry every 5 s (`WIFI_MANAGER_AP_SERVERS_RETRY_MS`): the implementers' choices.
- `WIFI_MANAGER_SCAN_GAP_MS` 20,000 and `WIFI_MANAGER_LIST_STALE_MS` 60,000 (plan C10b, 4.4); `AP_TAIL_FINISH_MS` 2,000 and `AP_TAIL_FINISH_MIN_MS` 5,000 (plan 4.6).
- The page (`code.js`): polls every 3.8 s, 950 ms while a Connect is decided; 30 s connect timeout; `No networks found` after 8 s; 60 s idle; 8 s request timeout; 15 s after Finish before the page resumes.

**For the user's decision**
1. **PH-7:** after a success, "Other Network" can start a switch late in the 60 s tail, and the setup SoftAP can stop while the user is still typing (a Connect sent in time is safe). The same trap in the router-fallback portal right after an automatic rejoin (a 20 s tail). Recommendation (fixer and council): keep the button for CP7 and watch P-8 and P-9; if users hit it, show the remaining time or relabel the button "Back".
2. **Before any push** (no amend or rebase here): six subjects exceed 72 characters, with rewords proposed: `e7a7c9a` "feat(portal): add Finish, stopping the setup AP 2 s after the tap (C12)"; `d616bb2` "fix(portal): announce channel switches, scan gently, hint channels (C13)"; `93f7162` "feat(portal): rebuild the setup page on status.json and Finish (C9)"; `f84500e` "fix(portal): harden connect ownership against late events, stale scans"; `2b3a501` "fix(portal): let a later status reply pick the view if the first is lost"; `ffd06ba` "fix(portal): copy the list with a small slack; send under the lock last". Three bodies carry a wrong figure: `86072f0` (`code.js` at `6c82af4` is 5,392 B gzipped, not 5,409), `35d3192` (`wifi_manager.c` `.text` +42, not +31), `802903f` (`.text` and `.literal` unchanged, not +8 / +4). With `9583236`'s reword and `6b84ae3`'s password redaction.
3. **The open setup SoftAP** (pre-existing, made no worse; D9 kept): `CONFIG_DEFAULT_AP_PASSWORD` has 7 characters, so the SoftAP is open. Anyone in range during a router outage can `DELETE /connect.json` (the hub forgets Wi-Fi and reopens the reset portal, BLE paused until WP8), or repeat `POST /connect.json`, each USER attempt taking a 7 s "portal submit" radio hold. A WPA2 SoftAP password, or a limit on submit holds per client, would close both: for WP8 and the user.

**Residual risks** (none blocks CP7; owner in brackets)
1. **A Connect that leaves a working network during the first-setup tail** closes the portal window ("Wi-Fi lost after setup"), so BLE resumes during the new attempt (errs toward leak protection; the submit's radio hold covers the attempt). [WP8 removes the window]
2. **An IP of a written-off attempt arriving after a new USER attempt started** is credited to the new one (the only guard found, checking the associated AP's SSID, risks hidden networks). [none]
3. **A forget whose disconnect call fails while an attempt then connects** is dropped when its wait expires; the page shows the truth, and a second Disconnect recovers. [none]
4. **A leave of an IP not to be kept that gets no disconnect event** keeps that link with a truthful status; a reboot then opens the BLE-paused reset portal when nothing is saved (also a replacing Connect whose post gets 503 while the first candidate connects: the page shows "Connected", Finish stops the AP, the next reboot opens the reset portal). [one line for WP8: D2 makes the reset portal keep scanning]
5. **A Connect whose IP lands exactly at its 25 s abort** is saved, dropped by our own disconnect, then rejoined by the router retry within about 30 s. [none]
6. **A full wifi_manager queue** (about a second) blocks the httpd task up to 200 ms per page-ordered scan. [none]
7. **Enterprise and OWE networks** are not marked in the list; a Connect to one fails with `Security not supported`; OWE rows ask for a password. [WP10 register]
8. **The 60 ms scan dwell** applies to connect and rejoin scans beside BLE coexistence: count reason 201 (NO_AP_FOUND) on a weak router (G7); `wifi_scan_config_t`'s own times could limit it to page scans. [G7]
9. **httpd's LRU purge** picks a just-accepted session (counter 0) first: a 6th parallel connection can reset one whose request is in flight, leaving the page without `code.js` or `style.css` on a first load. [G-CNA S1]
10. **Pre-existing page nits:** no `autocorrect="off" spellcheck="false"` on the password and hidden-SSID inputs (iOS can autocorrect a typed SSID); an empty Rescan leaves dead rows for up to 8 s; a resumed raw (Latin-1) SSID that has left the list is re-sent as UTF-8. [WP10 register]

**Closed by WP4:** 15h residuals 1 (the forget and a Submit in one attempt) and 2 (two unlocked writers of the STA config) by C8, 3 (the password copy's dead-store `memset`: a volatile wipe) and 4 (`portMAX_DELAY` posts) by C6/C8, and 10's socket half (5 sessions); 15e item 2 (the page round's open review findings, replaced by C8 and C9); 15i's note that C8 must keep a request kind for the tail policy (the CONNECT_STA callback now passes `kind | WIFI_MANAGER_CONNECT_NOT_STARTED`); the `reset_button.c` `execute_wifi_reset()` forget comment (the window part stays for WP8). 15i residual 5's "[WP4/WP10]" item was WP3's and is closed there (15q).

**What to watch at CP7** (15t has the order)
- **Build:** three `gz_asset.py: … ->` lines, `code.js 17973 B -> code.js.gz 5678 B (33 comment lines left out, 55 blank lines, the others trimmed)`, no `more than one 5760 B TCP send buffer` warning; a `gz_asset.py: FAILED` stops the build by design.
- **P-14 with a laptop on the SoftAP:** `curl -I http://10.10.0.1/` gives headers only with `Content-Encoding: gzip`, `Vary` and `Cache-Control: no-store`; `curl --compressed http://10.10.0.1/` renders; `curl -H "Accept-Encoding: identity" http://10.10.0.1/` gets 406; a foreign `Host` gets 302 with `Connection: close`. A LAN `curl` to the STA's IP during the tail is shut at accept (no 403 body, no hang), with one `httpd_txrx` W line.
- **First load on iPhone and Android (G-CNA S1):** the list fills about 2-3 s after the page appears (the first non-background `GET /ap.json` orders the scan: a deviation from the plan's "at `GET /`", so the assets load with the radio on the SoftAP's channel); `No networks found` only after 8 s; Rescan counts down 20 s. Every asset loads (a missing `code.js` is a dead page). One Android pass with mobile data on.
- **Connect (G8x, P-7):** `Wi-Fi setup page: Connect - attempt started`; a wrong password shows `Wrong password` within about 10-20 s with NVS unchanged, and the old router is rejoined by the router retry within about 40 s (PH-4 fixed); open network, a password with spaces or `%`, UTF-8 and Latin-1 SSIDs, a 64-hex key and a 7-character refusal; `user connect: the candidate got its IP - it is the network in use now, saved`, then a reboot rejoins it. `an IP on a network other than the one in use` or `the network in use is not saved` in a normal run is a finding.
- **Forget in each STA state:** idle gives `ORDER_DISCONNECT_STA: the STA is not connected - the saved network is erased now`; connected or connecting erases at the disconnect; after a reboot nothing is saved and `portal priority ON` follows (until WP8).
- **Finish (P-9):** `Wi-Fi setup page: Finish - the SoftAP stops N.N s after the IP` (at least 5.0 s after the IP), STOP_AP by max(IP + 5 s, tap + 3 s), then `portal priority OFF (AP stopped) - BLE scanning resumed` and the cloud admitted; the sign-in window closes by itself.
- **CSA (P-8):** the driver's `<connect>csa … csa_count:3`; whether each phone follows or drops (G0 decides 3 or 5); a phone that drops must re-join and re-probe within the 15 s left-tail.
- **E2 laptop flood (G3):** at most 5 sessions, the `session closed` W line at most once per 10 s with `httpd_txrx` lines beside it, allocfail unchanged, no reboot.
- **Stack:** `stack overflow in task httpd` or `in task wifi_manager` through G8x (commit and save), status polling at 950 ms and the forget-save fallback is a WP4 finding.

**Docs still to follow (WP10 register).** `MANUAL_TEST_PLAN.md`: G8x, G-CNA S1, P-7, P-13 and P-14 curl steps; T4-10 and G0's text that expects `SoftAP configured 1` or `first Connect/Disconnect request` (15d's table, 15k item 1); P-9 should read max(IP + 5 s, tap + 3 s) (PH-11); an NVS-full case for the forget fallback (G-FAULT/G8x); the component change register (every C6-C13 patch). R9: 15i residual 5's tag `[WP4/WP10]` belonged to WP3 (done there).

### 15s. WP3's valve items, WP5 (one BLE scan executor) and WP6 (NORMAL de-lock, claims, persisted PHY, widened LR trigger) (2026-10-06)

**Why.** Plan §11: WP3's valve half (the stale-handle check and `REATTEMPT_COUNT` handling, red team SR-1, D6), WP5 (a single BLE scan executor, behaviour-equivalent), WP6 (NORMAL de-locked into N_CODED / N_MIXED with a 0-100 ms dither, the valve claim policy, per-sensor PHY bits persisted (D1: some field sensors are on 1M), and the widened leak-response trigger with its 10 min cap, D5). WP8's parts are left out on purpose: the AP modes, the JOIN/SUBMIT/RETRY/LIST pulses, the sensor-starvation guard, and the removal of the portal window and radio holds (they keep today's narrow trigger: a pended RMLEAK/CLOSE). The stream owned `main/ble_leak_scanner` and `main/ble_valve`.

**Commits** on `fix/2.1.4`: six implementer commits and nine review fixes, each compiled with the project's flags with NimBLE's connect re-attempt both on and off, no new warning (the known `app_ble_valve.c:106:9` stays on its line), no IRAM section. Nothing pushed, amended or rebased; CRLF kept.

| `d596424` | WP5 | `ble_leak_scan` is the only code that calls `ble_gap_ext_disc()` / `ble_gap_disc_cancel()`; the valve hunt is recomputed from facts (`ble_valve_hunt_wanted()`); every report goes to the leak sensors and to `ble_valve_note_adv()`, which only stores a claim request; the executor grants it (stops its scan, calls `ble_valve_claim_start()` on its own task). | `f04f2db` | WP6 | Every scan timed: N_CODED (1M 160/32 + Coded 160/128, 1 s), N_MIXED ([1M 1 s][Coded 1 s]), NORMAL_LR ([1M 1 s][Coded 0.6 s]), each next after U(0, 100 ms); a claim only in the dither after a scan that covered Coded; a 1.2 s Coded recovery after every claim; the plan's I1/I2 as `_Static_assert`s; PHY learned from `prim_phy`, carried across reloads by MAC, saved in `nvs_prov` namespace `ble_phy` (7 B per sensor, 112 B at most, at most once a minute, only when it differs); the 60 s `[SUMMARY]` line and the duty watchdog; NimBLE's log at WARN |
| `bc83df7`, `2be0d4f` | gates | The summary's stack high-water call removed (it would link an IRAM function); the two bench-dependent constants marked provisional |
| `3c65297` | F4, LEAK-3, F2 | `lr_poll()` reads `health_is_interlock_held()`, the rules engine's lock-free mirror of its latch (written at every latch change, `incident_save_to_nvs()` and `rules_engine_reset_all()`), instead of the rules mutex (up to 1 s per pass on the task that writes RMLEAK and CLOSE; a timeout dropped the confirmation and restarted the 10 min cap) |
| `5424512` | LEAK-8 | A DISCONNECT for a handle that is not the tracked link is logged and ignored |
| `bfe0e08` | F1 (major, two lists), F7, LEAK-6 | Confirmed in IDF 5.5.1 NimBLE: a scan's own LE Scan Timeout, processed late, resets whatever procedure NimBLE runs (`ble_gap_disc_complete()`), a claim's connect included, which then never completes. A claim is now granted only when a scan ends by itself (a hold's hunt too); nothing starts in the pass that cancels a scan (50 ms settle); each scan's callback argument is its generation; an end over 100 ms early is a stopped scan's late end (no claim, nothing starts before that scan's time is up); a refused cancel is retried every pass |
| `20a02ff` | LEAK-7 | A scan still running 1 s past its duration with no end event is cancelled and restarted |
| `9c9d293` | LEAK-1, F3 | Plan I2b on claims: at least 6 s + U(0, 1 s) of scanning since the last pulse, at most 12 s of pulses in any 60 s (a ring of 10), a leak response included |
| `7428375` | F2 | Only a claim that gives no link counts as failed; a link that reached CONNECT and dropped (0x3E included) neither counts nor resets the back-off (G6b, D6) |
| `643f2bb` | F1 backstop | A DISC_COMPLETE on the valve's handler while a connect is in flight, or a connect NimBLE does not run for 1 s on the same connect number, clears `g_connecting`, ends the claim and resets the BLE host (`ble_hs_sched_reset()`): the only public way to clear the controller's initiator. Should never print |
| `d674c1c` | F5, LEAK-5, F3 | The executor's slow chores run only right after a scan of 1 s or more starts (never after NORMAL_LR's 0.6 s Coded scan); a scan's end is stamped by the GAP handler and the dither and claim freshness count from it |
| `cc2a09b` | F6 | A known PHY changes only after 4 adverts in a row on the other PHY (the count in the spare bits of the per-sensor byte; not saved) |

**How it works now** (at `cc2a09b`): the executor (`ble_leak_scan`, priority 6) runs one pass at every notification (a scan's end, a kick from the valve module) and at least every 500 ms; each pass re-reads every fact: the portal window and the radio holds (`app_wifi_portal_priority_active()`, `app_wifi_radio_hold_active()`: lock-free reads), a connect in flight, NimBLE's sync, a recovery due, `ble_valve_lr_active()`, any known-1M sensor. Modes, first match: CLAIM (a connect in flight), PAUSED (not synced, or a hold with no hunt), HOLD_HUNT (a hold while a leak response is pended: the narrow CP6 exception, `portal_holds_valve()`), RECOVERY, NORMAL_LR, N_MIXED, N_CODED. In a hold the executor notices within 500 ms (as CP6's loop did; the router retry's 0.5 s lead covers it). The valve module's command task keeps its own per-pass checks (`link_poll()`, `claim_held_poll()`, `lr_poll()`, `portal_priority_poll()`); a hold's cancel of a claim's connect reads as `BLE_HS_EAPP` and is never counted as a failure.

**Log lines** (new or changed; none prints a credential; `BLE_VALVE: [HOST] NimBLE host task started`, the production tool's marker, is unchanged, and no new line matches its version or Wi-Fi MAC patterns):
- `BLE_LEAK` I: `Scan mode N_CODED: 1M 20 % + Coded 80 %, 1 s scans, each next after 0-100 ms` (and the N_MIXED and NORMAL_LR lines); `Valve hunt scan started (1M + Coded PHY, passive)`; `eleak <MAC> PHY learned: %s (was %s)`; `PHY table loaded: %u of %u sensor(s) known, %u on 1M`; `PHY table saved: %u sensor(s) known, %u on 1M`; `[SUMMARY] modes N_CODED %lu s, N_MIXED %lu s, NORMAL_LR %lu s, hold hunt %lu s, paused %lu s; BLE scanning %lu.%lu of %lu.%lu s (%u %); claims %u: pulses %lu.%lu s, recovery %lu.%lu s; PHY changes %u; adverts <id>=<n><C|M|?> …` every 60 s; `[CLAIM] Valve claim waits for the pulse-rate limit (I2b): …`; `Scan started after %u failed attempt(s)`. W: `Duty watchdog: BLE scanning ran %u % of its expected time for %u min (below 80 %)`; `Scan overdue: no end 1000 ms after its %u ms - stopping and restarting it`; `Scan ended after %lu of its %lu ms: a stopped scan's late end - the next starts after this one's time`; `PHY table not read from NVS …`, `PHY table not saved (%s) - retried later`.
- `BLE_VALVE`: `[CLAIM] Connecting to the valve: pulse up to %u ms[ (leak response pending)]`; W `[CLAIM] Valve claim failed (%s), %u in a row - next claim in %u s (no back-off while a leak response is pending)`; `[CLAIM] Valve link held 60 s - claim back-off reset (…)`; E `[CLAIM] NimBLE lost the valve connect in flight (%s) - resetting the BLE host`; W `[LR] Leak response pending, valve not linked (%s) - leak-response scanning, at most 600 s for this incident`, `[LR] Leak-response scanning resumed …`, `[LR] Leak-response scanning ended after %lu s (%s)`; I `[LR] The valve confirms the interlock for this incident (RMLEAK=1, CLOSED)`; `[DISCONNECT] Handle %u is not the tracked link (%u) - ignored`; the stale-handle and `Connect not started by this module` lines.
- Changed: `Extended passive scan started (1M + Coded PHY)` prints only when scanning resumes (boot, after a pause), not at every start. NimBLE's own INFO lines (`GAP procedure initiated: extended discovery` at every scan start, about 86,000 a day) are gone (tag at WARN).

**Memory** (objects at `-Og`, `545b8f2` against `cc2a09b`, the regenerated `sdkconfig.h` on both sides; matches the fixer's and the council's):

| Object | Code (`.text` + `.literal`) | `.rodata` | `.bss` | `.data` |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `app_ble_leak.c` | +5,124 B | +1,720 B | +50 B | 0 |
| `app_ble_valve.c` | +2,506 B | +1,250 B | +29 B | +2 B (the stale-handle sentinel) |
| **Total** | **+7,630 B** | **+2,970 B** | **+79 B** | **+2 B** |

- Newly linked: `ble_hs_synced` (flash; discarded at CP5); `ble_hs_sched_reset` was linked already; `esp_random` already in IRAM; `__udivdi3` in ROM. IRAM 0.
- **Heap at rest** (strictly an I10 breach, for the user): `esp_log_level_set("NimBLE", ESP_LOG_WARN)` allocates a permanent tag node (about 32 B), and the first PHY save creates NVS namespace `ble_phy`, whose entry NVS keeps on the heap (about 30 B, allocated at every later boot's NVS init). No task, timer or queue added.
- **Stack** (`ble_leak_scan`, 3,072 B, canary-checked): task frame 208 B, `exec_summary` 368 B plus the logging chain (`_vfprintf_r` alone 800 B), `phy_save` 288 B plus the NVS and flash write chain (about 1.1-1.3 KB), `read_whitelist` 416 B: the council's estimates of the peak range from 1.8 to 2.3 KB, about 0.7 KB of margin at worst. T6-11 must measure it. The NimBLE host task (4,096 B): `process_leak_adv` 336 → 368 B, `ble_valve_note_adv` 96 B.

**The council** (NimBLE and RTOS, leak safety, RTOS and memory; on `4e22719` … `cc2a09b`): **3/3 SHIP, no blocking issue.** Checked against IDF 5.5.1 NimBLE: `ble_gap_rx_le_scan_timeout()` → `ble_gap_disc_complete()` resets the master state whatever its op (the backstop's premise), `ble_gap_master_reset_state()` keeps `cb` (the `g_connecting` guard covers a late end on the valve's handler), `ble_gap_ext_connect()` sets `M_CONN` before its HCI command, `accept_master_conn()` refuses a connect the host forgot (`ENOENT`); a connect that runs out reports `BLE_HS_ETIMEOUT`, this module's cancel `BLE_HS_EAPP`. No lost wake, no busy loop, priority 6 below the NimBLE host (21) and the Wi-Fi tasks. No path weakens leak → RMLEAK → CLOSE: the rules engine's posts are untouched, RMLEAK is replayed before CLOSE at setup, and while a leak response is pending claims skip the back-off, pulse 2.5 s and the hold still lets the hunt run. `health_is_interlock_held()` is written with every change of the latch (all 14 sites). Commit hygiene fine.

**Provisional constants for the bench**
- `N_SCAN_MS` 1,000 (N_CODED's scans, the dither period; G4's 24 h soak decides 1 s or 3 s) and `ADV_SMAX_MS` 448 (FW 1.1.0's longest advert spacing; G0's shortest burst `dT` per sensor must stay at or below 437.5 ms, or the I1 asserts are invalid).
- Plan-fixed but bench-sensitive: `BLE_VALVE_CLAIM_MS` 1,500 / `BLE_VALVE_CLAIM_LR_MS` 2,500; the back-off {10, 30, 60, 300} s and `CLAIM_HELD_MS` 60 s; `LR_OVERLAY_CAP_MS` 600 s; `RECOVERY_MS` 1,200 and `BLIND_MAX_MS` 2,800; N_CODED 1M 160/32 + Coded 160/128; `JITTER_MS` 100; `DUTY_WARN_PCT` 80 over 2 summaries; I2b 6,000 ms spacing and 12,000 ms per 60,000 ms.
- The implementers' choices: `STALE_LINK_CONFIRM_MS` 1,000; `SCAN_GONE_MS` 1,000; `PHY_SAVE_MIN_MS` 60,000; `EXEC_POLL_MS` 500; `SCAN_RETRY_MS` 50; `CHORES_LATE_MS` 5,000; `LR_POLL_MS` 1,000; `PHY_FLIP_LOG_MS` 10,000; `I2B_JITTER_MS` 1,000; `SCAN_SETTLE_MS` 50; `SCAN_EARLY_MS` 100; `SCAN_OVERDUE_MS` 1,000; `ORPHAN_CONFIRM_MS` 1,000; `CHORES_MIN_SCAN_MS` 1,000; `PHY_FLIP_ADVERTS` 4.

**For the user's decision**
1. **The back-off exemption during a leak response** (F3, LEAK-1): claims skip the 10/30/60/300 s back-off while a leak response is pending (the implementer read the plan's "unless LR" as the trigger; both reviewers read it as exempting LR from SERVE's deferral only). With I2b it costs at most about four 2.5 s pulses a minute, and a pended CLOSE waits at most 6-7 s of scanning per failed claim instead of up to 300 s. **Recommendation (fixer, council): keep it.** *(Phase 2, 15u: kept as decision B1 by the orchestrator under the user's instruction of 2026-10-06; for the user's review.)*
2. **The valve's re-find under N_CODED** (LEAK-2, F6): 1M gets 20 ms in every 100 ms against the valve's 500-700 ms adverts. The reviewers' model: power-on to `[SCAN] Target MAC matched` p50 about 5 s, p90 about 10-20 s, 19-30 % of power cycles over 10 s, and 5-9 % of 1.5 s pulses catch no advert and back off 10 s; the plan assumed 1-3 s. So the 100-power-cycle gate (relink within 10 s) and G6 (powered → linked within 5 s after the 10 min cap) will probably fail. Proposed, **not implemented without approval:** while the valve is wanted, unlinked and outside LR, run [N_CODED 1.0 s][1M 0.3 s] (I1 holds), and/or N_CODED's 1M at 176/35 to break the 5:1 harmonic; start the back-off only after 2 pulses in a row with no link. The leak-safety member also asks for it at least while a CLOSE is pended after the 10 min cap. *(Phase 2, 15u: built as decision B2 by the orchestrator, `e874bf9`, `183d361`, `d7a918e`, `96b09c0`: NORMAL hunts with [N_CODED 1.0 s][1M 0.45 s] while the valve is wanted and unlinked outside the LR overlay, backed off to every 6th period after 10 min; the back-off starts at the 2nd empty claim in a row. For the user's review, with the gate's wording.)*
3. **The unknown-PHY window after the 2.1.3 → 2.1.4 upgrade** (LEAK-4): until a 1M sensor is first heard it gets N_CODED's 20 % 1M (CP6 gave 50 %): a wetting then is detected late (15-30 s in a few % of cases; about 26 % of edge bursts missed whole), never missed. The plan allows it (I1). Recommendation: accept and say so in the release notes; option: N_MIXED while any listed sensor's PHY is unknown, for at most 10 min after boot or provisioning. *(Phase 2, 15u: built as decision B3 by the orchestrator: N_MIXED in NORMAL, AP_K1M in the AP modes, while a listed sensor's PHY is unknown, for at most 10 min after boot or after the list last gained a sensor; for the user's review. A 1M sensor first powered later still relies on N_CODED's 20 % until heard, M5.)*
4. **I10's heap exception** (about 62 B: the log-tag node and the namespace entry): approve until WP9's `CONFIG_BT_NIMBLE_LOG_LEVEL_WARNING` replaces the runtime call. *(Phase 2: `29f400b` (B4) made that replacement, so about 30 B remain, the `ble_phy` namespace entry; decision B4 by the orchestrator, 15u.)*
5. **Confirm `7428375`:** a link that reached CONNECT and was lost does not count as a failed claim (so G6b and D6's relink within 10 s can pass), or ask for a stricter rule. *(Phase 2, 15u: kept as decision B5 by the orchestrator; for the user's review.)*

**Residual risks** (none blocks CP7; owner in brackets)
1. **The claim path depends on the controller's LE Scan Timeout.** Timed extended scans never ran in 2.1.3 or CP6. If the controller stopped delivering it, every scan would end through `Scan overdue`, and no claim would ever be granted, even with a pended CLOSE. The event mask includes it and the controller is BLE 5.0, so low. [a later hardening: allow a claim after N overdue restarts; CP7 VAL-01 decides]
2. **A rejected scan geometry stalls its whole profile** (retried every 50 ms, no fallback). First on hardware: GEO_1M (160/160), the Coded-only scans of 0.6, 1.0 and 1.2 s, N_CODED's 32/128 split, the timed hunt (176/88). [CP7: run every mode once]
3. **A connect stuck inside NimBLE** (no answer to the pulse's Create Connection Cancel) leaves MODE_CLAIM with no scan and no executor-side limit; the duty watchdog does not count CLAIM time. The old 30 s connect had the same dependency, now met at every claim. [hardening: if CLAIM lasts more than 2.5 s + about 2 s, `connect_lost_by_host()`; count pulse overruns in the watchdog]
4. **Chores in MODE_CLAIM:** `k_prof[MODE_CLAIM].n == 0`, so the slow chores run on every pass of a pulse; a whitelist reload waiting up to 1 s on the provisioning mutex then delays RECOVERY after the CONNECT, and the Coded gap can reach the pulse + 1 s (about 3.5 s in LR, over I2's 2.8 s). Detection delay only. [one-line fix: leave MODE_CLAIM out of that condition, for the BLE owner in phase 2]
5. **Adverts at the tail of a stopped scan** in the late-end race reach the valve's handler while the claim's connect is the current procedure and are dropped (`[GAP] Unhandled event: 19`), not forwarded to `process_leak_adv()`. Rare. [a silent case for `DISC`/`EXT_DISC` there, or forwarding]
6. **The 10 min overlay counts from the incident's latch,** not from each new CLOSE: a CLOSE pended later in the same latch (a 24 h override expiring with the valve unlinked) gets no NORMAL_LR and relies on N_CODED's 20 % 1M; the back-off is still skipped. During an override the interlock is never confirmed (the valve reads OPEN), so claims skip the back-off whenever the valve is unlinked: more aggressive relinking, no safety cost. [with decision 2]
7. **`link_closed()` can run on two tasks** (host DISCONNECT, command-task stale path) with no exclusion: a DISCONNECT later than 1 s after NimBLE removed the link can post two `BLE_UPD_DISCONNECTED` and two `request_hunt()`. Cosmetic. [none]
8. **5 % of claims at marginal range:** after 4 pulses with no link the back-off reaches 300 s, so a marginal valve can stay offline past the 180 s CRITICAL threshold and raise a false valve-offline alert; a pending leak response bypasses the back-off. [field note]
9. **Dead or misleading code:** `CMD_WAKE_TOKEN` (sent only with the re-attempt option on, which the guard forbids) shares the 10-deep `ble_cmd_queue` with CLOSE/RMLEAK; the `s_scan_end_reason != 0` branch cannot run in IDF 5.5.1 (`ble_gap_disc_complete()` always reports 0); `app_ble_valve.c` still includes `rules_engine.h` (it keeps the line-106 warning where it is). [WP10 register]
10. **A provisioning read near its 1 s timeout** just as a 1 s scan starts delays the next scan once, widening one gap. [a short-timeout device-set read, the provisioning owner]

**Closed:** red team SR-1 (WP3's valve items); the two-owner `rc=2` scan race; 15o's request for a lock-free incident read on the valve task (done through the health mirror).

**What to watch at CP7** (15t has the order)
- **VAL-01 boot:** `BLE_LEAK: NimBLE ready, initializing scanner`, `Whitelist reloaded: N sensor(s)`, on boots after the first `PHY table loaded: K of N sensor(s) known, M on 1M`, then `Extended passive scan started (1M + Coded PHY)` and `Scan mode N_CODED: …` (N_MIXED with a known-1M sensor), then one `PHY learned: … (was unknown)` per sensor and `PHY table saved: …` (the first NVS write on this task: watch for a stack-canary panic). Never: `NimBLE: GAP procedure initiated`, a repeating `Failed to start ext scan`, `Scan not active (external cancel?)`, `Scan overdue`, `Duty watchdog`, `[GAP] Unhandled event: 8` or `19`, `[CLAIM] NimBLE lost the valve connect in flight`, `[HOST] NimBLE stack reset: reason=12`, `[DISCONNECT] Handle %u is not the tracked link`. Count `Scan ended after N of its M ms: a stopped scan's late end` (rare).
- **Every 60 s:** `[SUMMARY]` with BLE scanning at 90 % or more in NORMAL, `PHY changes 0`, every live sensor with a non-zero advert count within a few minutes (dry sensors burst about every 100 s), `claims N: pulses X s` with X at most N × 2.5 s (more means a connect stuck in NimBLE: send the log).
- **Every mode once:** N_CODED (boot), N_MIXED (a known-1M sensor), RECOVERY (any claim), NORMAL_LR (P11 with the valve unlinked: the only place its 1M 160/160 scan runs), HOLD_HUNT (the setup portal open plus a pended leak response). A rejected geometry shows as a repeating `Failed to start ext scan`.
- **P11, valve linked:** RMLEAK then CLOSE as before, then `[LR] The valve confirms the interlock for this incident (RMLEAK=1, CLOSED)`. **Valve not linked:** `[LR] Leak response pending, valve not linked (RMLEAK/CLOSE pended) - …`, `Scan mode NORMAL_LR …`, `[SCAN] Target MAC matched`, `[CLAIM] Connecting to the valve: pulse up to 2500 ms (leak response pending)`, `GAP CONNECT EVENT` status 0, `[LR] Leak-response scanning ended after N s (valve linked)` (before `SETUP COMPLETE`), then `Applying pending RMLEAK command=1` before `Applying pending valve command=0`, then the interlock line.
- **P14 and the 100 valve power cycles:** per cycle `[DISCONNECT] reason=0x08` → `[SCAN] Starting scan for provisioned valve` → `[SCAN] Target MAC matched` → `[CLAIM] Connecting … 1500 ms` → `GAP CONNECT EVENT` → `SETUP COMPLETE`. Log power-on → `Target MAC matched` (the re-find, decision 2) separately from `Target MAC matched` → `SETUP COMPLETE`. A lost link no longer backs off (`7428375`), so links need not be held 60 s.
- **G6b (0x3E induced):** no back-off, but I2b holds the next claim 6-7 s of scanning after the previous CONNECT; measure `[DISCONNECT] reason=0x23e` → `GAP CONNECT` status 0 → `SETUP COMPLETE` (close to the 10 s gate).
- **G2 NORMAL:** wetting → `eleak <MAC> — leak=1` within 5 s, none over 60 s; with the valve linked, CLOSE within 2 s. A 1M sensor wetted right after the upgrade boot, before its `PHY learned` line (decision 3).
- **Dead valve (G6-style):** `[LR] … at most 600 s`, exactly 600 s later `[LR] Leak-response scanning ended after 600 s (the cap per incident - normal scanning, claims go on)`; powering the valve later still gives the 2.5 s claim and RMLEAK then CLOSE.
- **T6-11:** `ble_leak_scan`'s high-water mark (3,072 B, priority 6) after at least one `PHY table saved` and one `[SUMMARY]`; the NimBLE host task's too.

**Docs still to follow (WP10 register).** `MANUAL_TEST_PLAN.md`: the new lines; the power-cycle and G6b protocol above; T6-11 with `ble_leak_scan` at priority 6; VAL-01's `.bss` expectation (+79 B for these objects); the release notes' upgrade window (decision 3).

### 15t. Integration of WP3, WP4, WP5 and WP6; 🔨 Build checkpoint 7, of `b651701` (2026-10-06; the procedure final for phase 3 on 2026-10-07)

**What was integrated.** 83 commits after `1707588`, all on `fix/2.1.4`, made by three streams in one working tree at the same time, each on its own files: WP3 core, 24 (`fa05390` … `f649193`, 15q); WP4, 44 (`146498c` … `9ba8e9c`, 15r); the BLE stream (WP3's valve items, WP5, WP6), 15 (`4e22719` … `cc2a09b`, 15s). Each stream ran its own review rounds, a fixer, and a 3-member council: 3/3 SHIP each. **The integrator made no firmware change**: no cross-stream defect was found that needed one. **`9ba8e9c` is this phase's last firmware commit** (its message is comment-only); this docs commit follows it. Phase 2 (WP7's lab image and WP8's `radio_policy`, 15u) followed that night, with `52ef6a2` its last firmware commit; **phase 3 (15w) followed in the morning of 2026-10-07: its last firmware commit, `b651701`, is CP7's**, and the procedure below is final for it (WB-BUILD-1: until this update the section still named `52ef6a2`, which lacks seven firmware commits).

**Checks** (all in `…\scratchpad\finish\integrator\`; nothing in `build\`, nothing built, linked or flashed)
- **All 30 project sources** (`main\`, `components\wifi_portal\`) compiled at `9ba8e9c` with `compile_commands.json`'s flags against a `sdkconfig.h` regenerated by kconfgen from the regeneration below: 30/30, and the only warnings are the four known ones (`app_ble_valve.c:106:9`, `app_lora.cpp:194:5` ×2, `app_lora.cpp:160:13`). Against today's `build\config\sdkconfig.h`, `main.c` stops at exactly six `#error`s (lines 44, 47, 50, 53, 56, 62). The only IRAM sections are the pre-existing ones (`lora.cpp` 10 B, `reset_button.c`'s 48 B ISR).
- **Link-level:** no global symbol is defined twice across the 30 objects, and every project-level reference resolves. New external references against `545b8f2` (`nm -u`): the three `_binary_*_gz_*` pairs; `ble_hs_synced`, `cJSON_PrintBuffered`, `cJSON_PrintPreallocated`, `esp_wifi_set_scan_parameters`, `httpd_sess_trigger_close` (flash, in CP5's discarded sections); `ble_hs_sched_reset`, `lwip_shutdown`, `strchr` (linked at CP5); `calloc`, `realloc`, `esp_random` (in IRAM since CP5). So DIRAM `.text` should stay 113,387 B.
- **The `sdkconfig` regeneration, simulated.** kconfgen 2.5.0 with the build's own `config.env`, `Kconfig` and renames, in scratch: run on today's `sdkconfig` it reproduces it byte for byte (SHA256 `98F3B2CC…AE759767`); run after the eight lines are deleted it differs in exactly the expected 18 lines (below), and the result's SHA256 is **`BFD5247E70D0E35B3246D9B498ECC709B10DD739CEE807291B5A4100C99F3AC8`**. **Superseded by WP8's core stage (`29f400b`, B4):** `sdkconfig.defaults` gains `CONFIG_BT_NIMBLE_LOG_LEVEL_WARNING=y` (NimBLE's log cap moved from a run-time `esp_log_level_set()` to sdkconfig, with an I14 guard in `main.c`), so the regeneration also deletes the six `CONFIG_BT_NIMBLE_LOG_LEVEL*` lines: rerun the same way (IDF_INIT_VERSION 5.5.1), it differs from the phase-1 result in exactly three more lines on each side (24 in all, below), SHA256 **`BF60B3864D4C8AD25A2329E719716CE6E8367E388FEF078C82CAB873F0E266B8`**. The 80 NimBLE host objects compiled both ways: code −2,540 B, `.rodata` −1,642 B, `.bss` 0, no warning. **Superseded again by WP7 (`434bda1`):** its Kconfig option `APP_RADIO_LAB` (default off) adds one line, `# CONFIG_APP_RADIO_LAB is not set`: 25 lines in all, SHA256 **`9E13270C4A2D05B0781A88841318160C588683CE06D1574935C17308AAE0412D`** (kconfgen 2.5.0 in scratch, run by the lab stage and again by the WP8 review; `sdkconfig.h` is unchanged by the lab line).
- **The IDF objects the seven lines touch**, compiled with both headers (NimBLE `ble_gap.c`, `ble_hs_hci_evt.c`, `ble_hs.c`, `ble_hs_conn.c`; mbedTLS's `ssl_*.c`, the dynamic-buffer port, `esp_tls*.c`, `esp_crt_bundle.c`; `wifi_init.c`; `mqtt_client.c`): no warning either way. `.bss` **−2,008 B** (`ble_adv_reattempt` 1,900, `ble_conn_reattempt` 100, `reattempt_conn` 8; all three are in CP5's map, at `0x3fcb00f0`, `0x3fcb085c`, `0x3fcafe64`), code −1,491 B (NimBLE −1,772, `ssl_tls.c` +274 without the kept certificate), `.rodata` −287 B.
- **Cross-stream interfaces**, each read at both ends: the executor and `app_wifi.c`'s holds (`app_wifi_portal_priority_active()`, `app_wifi_radio_hold_active()`, lock-free reads, polled at least every 500 ms); `main.c`'s I5 guard and the valve module's `#if MYNEWT_VAL(BLE_ENABLE_CONN_REATTEMPT)` (compiled out); WP3's rules changes and the valve module (`ble_valve_cancel_pending_close()`, `ble_valve_get_rmleak_state()` lock-free, `health_is_interlock_held()` written at every latch change, including the owed purge's release and `rules_engine_reset_all()`); `provisioning_get_rules_and_state()`'s new signature (rules engine only) and `provisioning_get_summary()` (the twin and lifecycle builders, `iothub_task` only; the executor reads `provisioning_get_device_set()`); the CONNECT_STA callback's `kind | NOT_STARTED` (WP4's two files only); the channel line against C13 (finding 1); the production tool's markers (`Firmware version: v…`, `Gateway ID :`, `WiFi STA MAC:`, `Initializing LoRa Driver...`, `BLE_VALVE: [HOST] NimBLE host task started`: all unchanged, and no new line matches its version or Wi-Fi MAC patterns: `[SUMMARY]`'s `recovery 1.2 s` has "ver" but no dotted version after it).

**Interaction findings** (from running the three streams' changes together; none needs a code change for CP7)
1. **WP3's F1 does not hold: channel 11 reaches every hub.** The WP3 cloud member (major) and both later WP3 votes said the NVS "settings" blob's `ap_channel` (1 on hubs set up before) overrides `CONFIG_DEFAULT_AP_CHANNEL` 11, and asked the portal owner to force it after the blob load. In the code (`wifi_manager.c`), the task's start configures the SoftAP once (`esp_wifi_set_config(ESP_IF_WIFI_AP, …)` at about line 2204, `.channel = wifi_settings.ap_channel`, still the RAM default 11) and only then posts `WM_ORDER_LOAD_AND_RESTORE_STA`, whose `wifi_manager_fetch_wifi_sta_config()` copies the blob into `wifi_settings`; nothing configures the SoftAP again (START_AP only switches the mode to APSTA), and the driver keeps no config of its own (`WIFI_STORAGE_RAM`). WP4's comment at `app_wifi.c:585-587` says so, and its AP-start line now prints `DEFAULT_AP_CHANNEL`, which is what the driver was given. **Corrected bench expectation:** after a 10 s reset (no router joined since boot) `Wi-Fi channel at AP start: radio 11 (SoftAP configured 11), router not joined since boot` on every hub; with a router joined, the radio follows the router's channel (plan §4.5), so `radio` may differ from `configured`. The WP3 notes "expect `SoftAP configured 1`" and "expect channel 1 on any hub that ever saved Wi-Fi" are wrong. The stored blob still holds 1 on older hubs; it is read and never applied. [WP10 register: a comment where the blob is loaded, so that no later change re-applies `wifi_settings` to the SoftAP]
2. **The `.bss` gate is comfortably met.** The objects add +155 B (WP3 core +19, WP4 +57, BLE +79), so the gate (plan §10, ≤ +450 B against `520b17a`, with WP2c's and WP2d's approved exceptions) reads +278 B; the re-attempt line's −2,008 B brings it to **about −1.73 KB**. 15p's "13 B short for WP9" is gone, with about 2.2 KB of room for WP7-WP8.
3. **Build order: CP6 has no guards.** A build of `545b8f2` after the regeneration below silently uses the new values (re-attempt off, 16/16 buffers, 2 KB TLS records, channel 11). If CP6 is still wanted, build it first, or in a worktree of `545b8f2` with a copy of today's `sdkconfig` (`98F3B2CC…AE759767`); never after the regeneration. Keep the regeneration's backup.
4. **Holds and the executor** (WP4's `app_wifi.c`, the BLE stream's executor): a hold that starts is noticed within the executor's 500 ms poll, as CP6's 500 ms loop did; the router retry's 0.5 s lead (`RADIO_HOLD_RETRY_LEAD_MS`) covers it, so a Wi-Fi scan or connect can still meet the last few milliseconds of a BLE scan. Not a regression. A kick from `app_wifi.c` at a hold's start would remove it; WP8's `radio_policy` replaces both. [WP8]
5. **Priorities.** `ble_leak_scan` now runs at 6, above `iothub_task`, `wifi_task`, `ble_valve`, the wifi_manager task (5; 8 in the portal window) and httpd (5). Its passes are short; its longest CPU stretch is the 60 s `[SUMMARY]` line (about 300 characters), which on UART0 with no TX buffer (15n R2) keeps its core about 26 ms once a minute; a leak evaluated on the other core is unaffected, on the same core it waits behind it. Same class as 15n R2. [D4's measurement at CP7]
6. **The BLE host reset backstop and the rules engine.** `643f2bb`'s `ble_hs_sched_reset()` drops the valve link: the DISCONNECT path runs as at any link loss, pended RMLEAK and CLOSE stay pended and are replayed RMLEAK first at the next link; the health mirror the valve task reads is the rules engine's own. Consistent; the reset should never happen.
7. **NVS.** The PHY table lives in `nvs_prov` (namespace `ble_phy`, one blob of at most 112 B, written at most once a minute and only on change), beside provisioning, sensor metadata, the rules latch, the DPS cache and the hub identity (16 KB, 4 pages; the BLE council counts about 90 live entries of about 378). WP4 keeps the default partition's Wi-Fi layout. A 10 s reset erases Wi-Fi only (the PHY table is kept, by design); a decommission drops a sensor's PHY at the next whitelist reload; `nvs_store.c`'s partition erase clears it with everything else.
8. **Heap at the portal.** The new `sdkconfig` values only lower ceilings (16 RX / 16 TX dynamic Wi-Fi buffers: the E2 flood holds at most about 54 KB, not 109 KB); no TLS runs while the SoftAP is up (D4), so the 2 KB records and the freed certificate matter only after it stops. The BLE changes add no heap use beyond about 62 B at rest (15s decision 4); WP4 saves 151 B at rest. WP4's 12 KB session gate is unchanged by the other streams.
9. **The AP tail, Finish, cloud admission and the MQTT stop.** The cloud is never admitted while the SoftAP is up, so no MQTT stop (WP3's retries on `wifi_task`, up to about 200 ms) can run during an AP tail; Finish's stop is a timer re-arm that never waits. No conflict.
10. **`httpd_txrx` W lines** (WP4's `open_fn` `shutdown()`): one `httpd_sock_err: error in recv : 128` per refused session, a tag `main.c` does not cap. Expected on the bench (15r); capping it in `main.c` would add a permanent tag node (I10), so it is left. [WP10 or WP9]
11. **Docs consistency:** 15j's "`sdkconfig` unchanged" and the untracked `CP6_FULL_TEST_PLAN.md`'s B5 hold for CP6 only; T4-10's and G0's `SoftAP configured 1` and the old activity names (`Connect/Disconnect request`, `network list request`) change at CP7; 15k's "Report at once" list must not count esp-mqtt's `Client asked to stop, but was not started` when the new `MQTT stop refused …` line follows it (15q).

**The streams' requests to each other, and what became of them**
- WP3 core → the portal owner: force `wifi_settings.ap_channel = DEFAULT_AP_CHANNEL` after the blob load and log the configured channel. **Not applied:** its premise does not hold (finding 1); the AP-start line already prints the configured channel.
- WP3 core → the valve stream: never reference `event->reattempt_cnt` unguarded. **Already so:** `app_ble_valve.c:2177` is under `#if MYNEWT_VAL(BLE_ENABLE_CONN_REATTEMPT)`; it compiles with the option on and off.
- WP3 core → `sensor_meta.c` / `hub_identity.c` (optional): reject control characters at intake. **Not applied:** optional, and the output is sanitised (15q residual 9).
- BLE → the rules engine: a lock-free read of the incident latch. **Done in the BLE stream** through the health engine's existing mirror (`3c65297`); no rules change needed.
- BLE → `main.c`: move the NimBLE log cap next to `main.c`'s caps. **Not applied:** no functional difference; WP9 replaces the runtime call with `CONFIG_BT_NIMBLE_LOG_LEVEL_WARNING`.
- BLE → the provisioning owner: a device-set read with a short timeout for the executor's whitelist reload. **Not applied:** not a defect (15s residual 10); for WP8 with the executor's chores.
- WP4 → the production-tool owner: the `ver` rule against `session closed: … since the server start`. **Checked:** the tool's pattern needs a dotted version after `version`, `ver` or `fw`, and none follows; that line never prints at a production boot anyway.
- Every request to the docs owner is applied in 15q-15t, the CHANGELOG and the plan's notes; `MANUAL_TEST_PLAN.md` items are in each section's WP10 register.

**For WP8 (phase 2), from the three streams**
- The CONNECT_STA callback's parameter is `kind | WIFI_MANAGER_CONNECT_NOT_STARTED` (USER 1, AUTO 2, RESTORE 3, APP_RETRY 4; USER starting gives `submit_in_flight`); `wifi_manager_scan_request(rescan, &wait_ms)` is the single hook to turn into LIST pulse requests; the activity hook's `API_USER` covers `GET /ap.json` without `?bg=1`, `POST /scan.json` and `POST /finish.json`, `API_BG` is `GET /ap.json?bg=1` (status polls carry `?bg=1` and stay STATUS); `page_in_use()` keys the router retry's deferral; the page chain's BLE-window code is effectively dead since C10b's 20 s gap: delete it with the holds (15r).
- Drive the executor through `ble_valve_hunt_wanted()`, `ble_valve_claim_wanted()` / `ble_valve_claim_start()`, `ble_valve_lr_active()` / `ble_valve_lr_pending()` and `app_ble_leak_kick()`; add the AP modes to `app_ble_leak.c`'s profile and mode tables; apply the widened trigger to LR_AP and delete `portal_holds_valve()` with the holds (15s). Kick the executor when a hold starts (finding 4), leave MODE_CLAIM out of the chores' condition (15s residual 4), and bound MODE_CLAIM (15s residual 3).
- D2 (leak protection in the reset portal) also closes 15r residual 4's reboot case and the open-SoftAP forget of 15r decision 3.

**🔨 Build checkpoint 7: the procedure, of `b651701`** (phase 3's last firmware commit; everything after it is docs, so a build of any later commit on `fix/2.1.4` is the same image: `git diff --stat b651701 HEAD -- main components sdkconfig.defaults CMakeLists.txt partitions.csv dependencies.lock` must print nothing; final 2026-10-07; this is what it adds to CP6's procedure). In PowerShell, with the ESP-IDF 5.5.1 environment, in the project folder. Save the last build's reference first, since `fullclean` deletes `build\`. **The regeneration is a one-time step on this machine:** `main.c`'s guards stop any build with today's `sdkconfig`; do not delete `sdkconfig` itself (`sdkconfig.defaults` has no target, flash size, partition table or BT settings). **Keep the pre-regeneration `sdkconfig` outside the repo** (UPG-1): the 2.1.3 baseline worktree of `MANUAL_TEST_PLAN.md` 0.4 must be built with it, never with the regenerated file, which carries 2.1.4's values that 2.1.3 has no guards for.

Select-String -Path build\eFloStop_WiFiHub_idf1.bin -SimpleMatch -Quiet -Pattern 'SUBMIT pulse not granted before its Connect ended'

**Expected**
- **Before:** `git log --oneline -1` shows `b651701` or a later commit, and the `git diff --stat` prints nothing (a later commit with a line there is a firmware change this procedure does not cover: stop). `sdkconfig`'s hash `98F3B2CC…AE759767` (CP5's; CP6 does not change it), 2,841 lines; `..\sdkconfig.pre_cp7` is that same file (`Get-FileHash ..\sdkconfig.pre_cp7`). `git status --short` lists only the usual entries (`.vscode/settings.json`, the two 2.1.3 docs, `.adsum/`, `main/.adsum/`, `docs/telemetry/app_req_v5/`, `docs/telemetry/build_app_requirements_v5.py`, and `docs/field_logs/2.1.4/CP6_FULL_TEST_PLAN.md` while it is untracked). If the hash is **`3B5BDCEE…C0E209`** (2,842 lines), a configure already ran at a phase-2 commit: it added only `# CONFIG_APP_RADIO_LAB is not set` and the build stopped at the guards; step 1 still applies and ends at the same file, but `Compare-Object` then shows 24 lines (the lab line is in both).

- **`Compare-Object` lists exactly these 25 lines** (`=>` new, `<=` old; the order may differ):
  - `=>` (12) `# CONFIG_BT_NIMBLE_ENABLE_CONN_REATTEMPT is not set`, `CONFIG_ESP_WIFI_DYNAMIC_RX_BUFFER_NUM=16`, `CONFIG_ESP_WIFI_DYNAMIC_TX_BUFFER_NUM=16`, `CONFIG_MBEDTLS_SSL_OUT_CONTENT_LEN=2048`, `# CONFIG_MBEDTLS_SSL_KEEP_PEER_CERTIFICATE is not set`, `CONFIG_DEFAULT_AP_CHANNEL=11`, `CONFIG_ESP32_WIFI_DYNAMIC_RX_BUFFER_NUM=16`, `CONFIG_ESP32_WIFI_DYNAMIC_TX_BUFFER_NUM=16`, (`29f400b`) `CONFIG_BT_NIMBLE_LOG_LEVEL_WARNING=y`, `# CONFIG_BT_NIMBLE_LOG_LEVEL_INFO is not set`, `CONFIG_BT_NIMBLE_LOG_LEVEL=2`, and (`434bda1`, WP7's lab option, off) `# CONFIG_APP_RADIO_LAB is not set`;

  - Anything else needs a look before the build. **The new hash should be `9E13270C4A2D05B0781A88841318160C588683CE06D1574935C17308AAE0412D`** (kconfgen 2.5.0 in scratch with the build's own `config.env`, `Kconfig` and renames and `IDF_INIT_VERSION` 5.5.1: by the lab stage, by the WP8 review, and again by the docs owner at HEAD on 2026-10-07, from a copy of today's `sdkconfig` put through this exact PowerShell filter; the same tool reproduces today's file byte for byte apart from the lab line, and regenerating the new file changes nothing). `BF60B386…E266B8` was `29f400b`'s alone, `BFD5247E…99F3AC8` phase 1's. Record it either way: it is the reference from CP7 on. `CONFIG_DEFAULT_AP_MAX_CONNECTIONS=4` is deleted and written back unchanged. **The boot (VAL-01)**, as CP6 (15j) plus:
- Unchanged and required: `HUB_IDENT: Firmware version: v2.1.4`, `Gateway ID : GW-…`, `WiFi STA MAC: …`, `AP SSID: WiFi-Hub-…`, the `bench build (APP_BENCH_DIAG)` warning, `BLE_VALVE: [HOST] NimBLE host task started`, `APP_LORA: Initializing LoRa Driver...`, `IOTHUB: cloud_tx started (stack 5120 B, priority 3)`. **No** `RADIO_LAB:` line (a production image).
- BLE (15s, 15u), in this order: `BLE_LEAK: NimBLE ready, initializing scanner`; **`RADIO: Profile self-test passed: 14 rows hold I1, I2, I8 and the period rule (SERVE rung SERVE-A)`** (16 rows, in its own wording, on the G1 lab image; `Profile self-test FAILED (…) - pinned to NORMAL (N_CODED), no Wi-Fi pulses` is a stop: send the log); `Whitelist reloaded: N sensor(s)`; `PHY table loaded: K of N sensor(s) known, M on 1M` (from the second CP7 boot on: **CP7's first boot has no PHY table**, as CP6 never saved one); then, once NimBLE syncs, `RADIO: Mode NORMAL (SoftAP down, or STA connected)` (`Mode BLE_IDLE (no BLE leak sensor listed; no valve, or its link is up) - no BLE scan` on a hub with no BLE sensor and its valve linked), `Extended passive scan started (1M + Coded PHY)` and the scan-mode line:
  - **first boot after the upgrade, or any listed PHY unknown:** `Scan mode N_MIXED (a sensor on 1M, or a sensor's PHY not known yet): 1 s on 1M and 1 s on Coded in turn, each next after 0-100 ms` (B3: at most 10 min after boot, ending sooner when every sensor is heard; it stays while a sensor is known on 1M), then one `eleak <MAC> PHY learned: Coded (was unknown)` per sensor and `PHY table saved: N sensor(s) known, M on 1M` (the first NVS write on this task: watch for a stack-canary panic);
  - **valve provisioned and not linked:** `Scan mode N_CODED with a valve hunt (valve not linked): 1 s of 1M 20 % + Coded 80 %, then 0.45 s on 1M, each next after 0-100 ms` (B2), then `[SCAN] Target MAC matched …`, `[CLAIM] Connecting to the valve: pulse up to NNNN ms` (1500-2500), `GAP CONNECT EVENT`, `SETUP COMPLETE - READY FOR GATT`, and `Scan mode N_CODED: 1M 20 % + Coded 80 %, 1 s scans, each next after 0-100 ms`.
  - No `NimBLE:` INFO line except `ogf=0x…, ocf=0x…, hci_err=0x… …` when an HCI command fails (`CONFIG_BT_NIMBLE_PRINT_ERR_NAME` keeps those, and each is a finding in itself); `GAP procedure initiated: …` is gone.
  - **Every 60 s, two `RADIO: [SUMMARY]` lines** (the single `BLE_LEAK: [SUMMARY]` of 15s is gone): `[SUMMARY] modes NORMAL N s, NORMAL_LR N s, AP_IDLE N s, SERVE N s, LR_AP N s, BLE_IDLE N s, paused N s; BLE scanning X of Y s (Z %); LR overlay N s; PHY changes N; adverts <id>=<n><C|M|?> …` with Z ≥ 90 in NORMAL; and `[SUMMARY] pulses JOIN a, SUBMIT b, RETRY c, LIST d, CONNECT e, refused f: X s blind (at most Y s in 60 s), recovery R s; longest Coded gap N ms (0 over 2900 ms); joins j, join to lease L ms at most`, with Y ≤ 12.0 and `(0 over 2900 ms)` always.
  - **Never** (send the log): `RADIO: I2: BLE went N ms with no Coded scan (limit 2800 ms) - send this log` (one exception known since the whole-branch review, WB-CONC-1, open: within about 1 s after a valve claim's `[CLAIM] Connecting to the valve: pulse up to NNNN ms`, above all a failed one, N up to about 3,400 ms can come from the claim's pulse being sized to the whole I2 budget while its end reaches the executor late, through the console lock or the 500 ms poll; record N and the claim lines, and send the log all the same: it is the measurement the fix needs); `Duty watchdog`; either `[CLAIM] … still in flight 2 s past its pulse` or `[CLAIM] A connect this module no longer tracks … resetting the BLE host` line (c49bc9c's reset path has never run on hardware; if one appears, check `[HOST] NimBLE stack reset` and resync, and `Extended passive scan started` within about 1 s; a `[CONNECT] Connect not started by this module (NimBLE re-attempt) cancelled` right after it is its companion, not a second fault); a repeating `Failed to start ext scan` (a geometry the controller refuses); `Scan overdue`; `radio policy: SoftAP up/down by the Wi-Fi mode but …` (a stuck SoftAP fact); a stack-canary panic on `ble_leak_scan`, `wifi_manager`, `wifi_task`, `httpd` or `sys_evt`.
- Cloud (15q): with Wi-Fi saved, `Connected! IP`, `IOTHUB: cloud admitted 0.N s after the IP (internal DMA free X B, largest Y B)` with X about 50-53 KB (CP6's 48-51 KB plus the re-attempt tables) and `Connected to Azure IoT Hub!`; DPS and MQTT handshakes with no `esp-tls` write error, mbedTLS −0x7200 / −0x7780 or handshake failure (a TLS failure means no cloud alerts; local auto-close does not depend on it).
- Portal (15r finding 1; D2, 15u): after a 10 s reset, `APP_WIFI: SoftAP up with no saved Wi-Fi credentials (setup portal) - BLE scanning, if any, stays on beside it` and `Wi-Fi channel at AP start: radio 11 (SoftAP configured 11), router not joined since boot`, then `RADIO: Mode AP_IDLE (SoftAP up, STA not connected): Coded 0.6 s / Wi-Fi 0.3 s, discovery every 2 periods` (with sensors or a valve provisioned; nothing provisioned: no mode line, or `Mode BLE_IDLE`). **Gone for good** (`dd19d6b`): `portal priority ON/OFF`, `BLE_LEAK: Scan paused - Wi-Fi setup portal has the radio`, `Scan resumed - Wi-Fi setup portal closed`, `[PORTAL] …`, `Valve scan held …`, `Wi-Fi radio hold ON/OFF`, `HEALTH_ENGINE: BLE scanning paused/resumed`.
- At rest with Wi-Fi connected and the SoftAP down, the `MONITOR: heap:` line's `free` about 8.6-11.6 KB above CP5's in the same state (CP6's 5.2-6.2 KB, about 1.9 KB from phase 1's static changes less about 0.45 KB from phase 2's, and up to about 4 KB from the certificate freed after the handshake): record it.
