### 15u. Phase 2: WP8's `radio_policy` in production (D2), WP7's lab image, the orchestrator's decisions, six reviews, a fix round and a 5/5 council (2026-10-07)

**Why and how.** Plan §11's last two radio packages: **WP8** puts `radio_policy` in production (the modes, the profile table, the pulses and their budgets; D2: leak protection keeps running in the reset portal) and deletes the portal window, the Wi-Fi radio holds, the page chain and the health hold in one commit; **WP7** is the G1 lab image. They ran overnight on 2026-10-06/07 under the user's instruction ("Finish everything first and then we'll test tomorrow the build and then work on the issues. Remember to run adversarial workflows on the work areas that you are working on", then "continue with top quality in mind"). An orchestrator split the work into stages, each in its own scratch folder (`…\scratchpad\finish2\`): **core** (WP8's policy and executor, B2-B4), **wifi** (the Wi-Fi side wired in, the deletions), **lab** (WP7); then **six adversarial reviews** (leak safety, phones, RTOS, coexistence, memory and build, and the council's leak model on the rows as built), **one fix round**, **a five-member council**, and this docs and checkpoint stage. No hardware: nothing ran `idf.py`, nothing touched `build\`, nothing was flashed; every compile was a scratch compile with the project's flags. G0 and G1 data do not exist yet, so every bench-dependent value is the plan's provisional one, each a named constant (table below).

**Decisions taken by the orchestrator, not by the user.** The user said "finish everything" and did not decide these; the orchestrator took them so the night's work could finish, had each one reviewed, and records them here as its own. **Each needs the user's ratification** (B1, B2, B3 and B5 answer 15s's items 1, 2, 3 and 5; B4 answers item 4).

| # | Decision | As built (commits) | What the reviews and the council found | For the user |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **B1** | Keep the claims' back-off exemption while a leak response is pending | unchanged since `ab7eae8` | holds; at most about four 2.5 s claims a minute inside I2b; a pended CLOSE waits 6-7 s of scanning per failed claim instead of up to 300 s. With a valve that is heard but never links, claims then use nearly all of I2b's room (B1 interplay below) | ratify (recommended), or keep the back-off during a leak response and exempt it only from SERVE's deferral (the 15s reviewers' reading of the plan) |
| **B2** | The valve's re-find: while the valve is wanted and unlinked outside the LR overlay, NORMAL adds a 1M hunt slot; a claim counts as failed for the back-off only after 2 empty claims in a row | `e874bf9`, `183d361`; review fixes `d7a918e`, `96b09c0`. Row N_HUNT = [N_CODED 1.0 s][1M **0.45 s**] (0.3 s at first; the plan's fallback geometry since the model review). No hunt slot within 7 s (`VALVE_HEARD_HOLD_MS`) of the valve being heard or claimed, while a claim is due or while claims back off; every 6th period only (`RP_HUNT_EVERY_BO`) once the valve has been hunted 10 min, never while an incident is latched | the model (valve ADV_IND every 500-700 ms, 2.5 s claims): link p50 / p90 1.6 / 3.3 s, 2.1 / 4.9 s and 3.1 / 9.9 s at valve p_loss 0.1 / 0.3 / 0.5; over 10 s per cycle 0.07 % / 1.7 % / 9.9 %; so **P(all 100 power cycles relink ≤ 10 s) = 0.93 / 0.18 / 0.00**: the gate as worded is statistical, and slow relinks are mostly a late first hearing, not an empty claim. Leak cost while the hunt runs at full rate: a 1 s wetting .774 / .616 (N_CODED .906 / .773), persistent leak p99.9 16.5 / 32 s at p_loss 0.3 / 0.5 (G2's 60 s holds); backed off .879 / .740 and 4.0 / 17.0 s | ratify; **restate the gate** (e.g. at most 2 of 100 over 10 s, with the valve's adverts per 1M slot recorded); the bench picks 0.3 or 0.45 s |
| **B3** | A 1M share while any listed BLE sensor's PHY is unknown, for at most 10 min after boot or after the list last gained a sensor | in `c0a1a69` / `50e5048` (`RP_UNKNOWN_PHY_MS` 600 s): NORMAL runs N_MIXED, the AP modes AP_K1M | P(a live 1M sensor still unknown after the 10 min) 1.4e-9 / 3.6e-6 in N_MIXED, 2.1e-6 / 2.6e-4 in AP_K1M (p_loss 0.3 / 0.5); a 1M sensor in N_MIXED is covered as a Coded one (p99 3.5 / 16.5 s). **M5:** a 1M sensor first powered more than 10 min after boot or provisioning gets only N_CODED's 20 % 1M share until first heard (p99 60-90 s for that first hearing) | ratify; M5's optional code (N_MIXED for 110 s at most once per 600 s while the starvation fact reports a 1M or unknown sensor) after G0 |
| **B4** | NimBLE's log cap in `sdkconfig`: `CONFIG_BT_NIMBLE_LOG_LEVEL_WARNING=y` in `sdkconfig.defaults` (the IDF 5.5.1 symbol, `bt/host/nimble/Kconfig.in`), an I14 `#error` at `main.c:64-66`; the run-time `esp_log_level_set("NimBLE", …)` and its heap tag node deleted | `29f400b`, docs `35fa836` | NimBLE host code −2,540 B, `.rodata` −1,642 B, `.bss` 0, about 32 B more heap at rest. **MB-3:** `CONFIG_BT_NIMBLE_PRINT_ERR_NAME=y` still prints `ogf=…, ocf=…, hci_err=…` at INFO when an HCI command fails (IDF's modlog ignores the level there) | ratify; keep `PRINT_ERR_NAME` (recommended: the line is a useful diagnostic; turning it off changes the CP7 hash again) |
| **B5** | A link that reached CONNECT is not a failed claim | unchanged since `7428375` | holds (G6b and D6's relink within 10 s can pass) | ratify |
| **SUBMIT** | The setup page's Connect (SUBMIT pulse) is always honoured and exempt from I2b's 6 s spacing, but **counts toward I2b's 12 s per rolling 60 s and toward I2's 2.8 s + 1.2 s**, so no request pattern blinds BLE beyond the invariants. While a leak response is pending, a due valve claim goes first and keeps 2.5 s of I2b's room (every Wi-Fi kind since `2f70ad8`) | `c0a1a69`, `95626db`; fixes `2f70ad8`, `f0bd523` | I1, I2, I2b and the claim-first rule hold under every pattern the models tried (100 fuzz seeds, 40 Connect-flood seeds, LEAK-1's attack). **M4 residual:** a flood aligned to the sensors' 15 s heartbeats fits I2b exactly (4 × 2.8 s a minute) and pushes the other sensors past G2's 60 s in SERVE-A (p99.9 62 s / 137 s at p_loss 0.3 / 0.5) | ratify; before release, M4's rule (a SUBMIT within 30 s of another takes the 6 s spacing; a person's Connect keeps its immediate pulse) or a per-client Connect limit; WPA2 stays excluded by the user. **Phase 3 (15w): M4 is closed in the model by `4e8a8cd`**: one stamp for SUBMIT and JOIN; one within 45 s of the last SUBMIT or JOIN pulse is paced (1.5 s from a Coded window's end, after the spacing); worst p99.9 30.5 / 47.5 s. Ratify in 15x |

One more extension by the core stage, also the orchestrator's: **a valve claim outside a leak response lasts 1.5-2.5 s in NORMAL** (clipped to the I2 budget; the plan says 1.5 s); since `1e39cdb` it is exactly 1.5 s in AP_IDLE (none while a station joins), and exactly 2.5 s under a leak response (plan). Keep it for CP7 and decide on the bench numbers; the plan's length is a one-line change in `radio_policy_exec_connect_len()` (NORMAL's `rp_min(RP_CONNECT_LR_MS, budget_ms)` → `RP_CONNECT_MS`).

**`52ef6a2` is phase 2's last firmware commit; CP7 was of it (15t).** This stage's commits are docs only. *(Superseded by phase 3, 15w: seven firmware commits follow, and CP7 is of `b651701`.)*

**How it works now** (`main/radio_policy/radio_policy.h` documents every fact, row and constant)
- **One source of the radio mode.** `radio_policy` keeps facts with one writer each (the SoftAP and the STA's IP from the wifi_manager callbacks; a STA attempt and the page's Connect; the SoftAP's stations from the default event loop, pruned against the driver's list every `wifi_task` pass; the page's activity from httpd's hook; the leak-response trigger from the valve module). The BLE executor (`ble_leak_scan`, priority 6) asks it for the mode and row on every pass, at least every 500 ms. Modes, first match: PAUSED (NimBLE not synced), BLE_IDLE (no BLE leak sensor listed, and either no valve or the valve linked), LR_AP / NORMAL_LR (a leak response with the valve unlinked, at most 10 min per incident; SoftAP up and STA not connected, or not), NORMAL (also the TAIL: SoftAP up with the STA connected), SERVE (a page used in the last 60 s, a Connect in flight, or 60 s after a new lease once per station per 10 min), AP_IDLE.
- **14 rows**, each checked by `_Static_assert`s (I1, I2, I8, the period rule, and since `52ef6a2` that every row but RECOVERY is checked for I1) and again by the boot self-test, which pins NORMAL (N_CODED) with no Wi-Fi pulse if any check fails: N_CODED [N 1.0], N_HUNT [N 1.0][M 0.45], N_MIXED [M 1.0][C 1.0], NORMAL_LR [M 1.0][C 0.6], RECOVERY [C 1.2], SERVE_A [C 0.6][W 0.6] and its discovery row, SERVE_B and its discovery row (selectable rung), APIDLE [C 0.6][W 0.3] and its discovery row, AP_K1M [C 0.6][W 0.3][M 0.6][W 0.3], LR_AP_30 [M 1.2][C 0.6], LR_AP [M 0.6][W 0.3][C 0.6][W 0.3]. NORMAL's rows are dithered (each scan after U(0, 100 ms)); a row switch starts at its Coded window (I1); an I8 row entered right after a 1M slot leads with a 0.3 s Wi-Fi slot when I1 allows it; a Wi-Fi slot is timed from the end of the slot before it.
- **Pulses** (the only Coded stops longer than a row's own gap): JOIN (a station joins; ends at max(lease + 1.5 s, first 302 or page + 0.3 s), the budget, or the station leaving; per station 30 s + U(0, 10) s apart; asked up to 10 s while the station settles; a second station's assist is owed and asked later), SUBMIT (the page's Connect; always honoured), RETRY (the router retry, 1.5 s, only at a Coded window's end), LIST (the hub's network-list scan, at most 2.5 s, ended by `cb_scan_done`), CONNECT (a valve claim, granted at a scan's own end). Every pulse is clipped to I2's 2.8 s from the last Coded window and followed by a 1.2 s Coded recovery with no grant; every pulse counts in I2b's 12 s per rolling 60 s (16 buckets of 4 s, which can only over-count); all but SUBMIT need 6 s + U(0, 1 s) of profile time since the last. Under a leak response a due claim goes first; JOIN, RETRY and LIST get no pulse in LR_AP's first 30 s, then one JOIN per 60 s. Requesters never hold a lock while they wait; a RETRY or LIST not granted in 2 s goes on beside BLE with a W line. The executor expires every pulse itself (I3); a claim's connect still in flight 2 s past its pulse resets the BLE host (`c49bc9c`).
- **Requesters** (`app_wifi.c`): `list_scan_gate()` on the wifi_manager task right before each `esp_wifi_scan_start()` (refused below 24 KB of internal DMA heap, while a station joins (I7) or while a STA attempt is in flight); `router_retry()` on `wifi_task` (30 s + U(0, 5) s after the last attempt, deferred while the page is used, at most 10 s for a joining station). `radio_policy_portal_activity()` is called first in the portal's activity hook.
- **D2.** The reset portal (no credentials) runs AP_IDLE, SERVE or LR_AP whenever sensors or a valve are provisioned: BLE leak detection and the valve's hunt go on during setup. The council's leak model for the reset portal in AP_IDLE: persistent-leak p99.9 4 s / 17.5 s at p_loss 0.3 / 0.5, detected within 60 s with probability 1.0000; before D2 the portal window paused it all.

**Deleted** (`dd19d6b`, one commit, as plan §11 requires): `app_wifi.c`'s portal priority window (and its wifi_manager priority raise), every Wi-Fi radio hold, the page chain, QUIET and the long listen, and the `cb_scan_start` scan-stop path; `main/app_wifi/portal_priority.h`; `health_set_ble_scan_paused()` and `health_note_valve_leak_hunt()` (`health_engine.{c,h}` are byte-identical to `5b5d70e~1` again: blobs `3857e9f`, `5dad08a`); the executor's HOLD mode, the HOLD_HUNT row and slot kind H; the valve's `portal_holds_valve()`, `portal_priority_poll()` and `[PORTAL]` lines; the `reset_button.c` window comment. The phase-1 `BLE_LEAK: [SUMMARY]` line became the two `RADIO: [SUMMARY]` lines (`50e5048`).

**Log lines** (none prints a credential; none matches the production tool's version or Wi-Fi MAC patterns: every added string literal checked, and the markers `Firmware version: v…`, `Gateway ID :`, `WiFi STA MAC:`, `Initializing LoRa Driver...` and `BLE_VALVE: [HOST] NimBLE host task started` are unchanged)
- New tag `RADIO`: `Profile self-test passed: 14 rows hold I1, I2, I8 and the period rule (SERVE rung SERVE-A)` / E `Profile self-test FAILED (%s: %s) - pinned to NORMAL (N_CODED), no Wi-Fi pulses`; one mode line per change (`Mode NORMAL (SoftAP down, or STA connected)`, `Mode AP_IDLE (SoftAP up, STA not connected): Coded 0.6 s / Wi-Fi 0.3 s, discovery every 2 periods`, `Mode SERVE (…): Coded 0.6 s / Wi-Fi 0.6 s (rung SERVE-A), discovery every 3 periods`, `Mode BLE_IDLE (…) - no BLE scan`, `Mode PAUSED (NimBLE not synced) - no BLE scan`, W `Mode LR_AP (…)`, W `Mode NORMAL_LR (a leak response, the valve not linked)`); pulse lines (`JOIN pulse for station <MAC>: BLE off for up to N ms`, `<KIND> pulse: BLE off for up to N ms`, `<KIND> pulse over after N ms (its deadline | its requester ended it | the Connect's outcome | the station left | its station is gone | its lease and first page or 302)`, `JOIN pulse over after N ms with no lease, beside a STA attempt - one more assist after the recovery and the pulse spacing`, W `<KIND> pulse not granted (<why>) - Wi-Fi goes on beside BLE`, at most one per 10 s, `<KIND> pulse waits: the valve's claim goes first (a leak response is pending)`, `[CLAIM] Valve claim waits for the pulse-rate limit (I2b): …`); `Discovery backed off: its reasons are 10 min old (…)`; W `Sensor-starvation guard: a sensor unheard 250 s or more (…) - <row> for 110 s` / `Sensor-starvation guard over`; the two `[SUMMARY]` lines; W `Duty watchdog: …`; E `I2: BLE went N ms with no Coded scan (limit 2800 ms) - send this log`.
- `BLE_LEAK`: `Scan mode N_CODED with a valve hunt (valve not linked): …, then 0.45 s on 1M, …` and `Scan mode N_CODED with a valve hunt, backed off (valve unlinked 10 min or more): … every 6 periods 0.45 s more on 1M, …`; `Scan mode N_MIXED (a sensor on 1M, or a sensor's PHY not known yet): …`. Gone: `Scan paused - Wi-Fi setup portal has the radio`, `Scan resumed - Wi-Fi setup portal closed`, the hold-hunt text.
- `BLE_VALVE`: W `[CLAIM] Valve claim failed (%s), %u in a row - the next needs only the pulse spacing, no back-off` (the first empty claim, B2); E `[CLAIM] NimBLE lost the valve connect in flight (still in flight 2 s past its pulse) - …` and E `[CLAIM] A connect this module no longer tracks (cancelled, or NimBLE's own) still runs 2 s past its pulse - resetting the BLE host` (never expected). Gone: `[PORTAL] …`, `[SCAN] Valve scan held …`.
- `APP_WIFI`: `SoftAP up with no saved Wi-Fi credentials (setup portal) - BLE scanning, if any, stays on beside it`; `Wi-Fi list scan not started: a station is joining the SoftAP - …` / `… a connect attempt is in flight - …` / W `… internal DMA free N B (needs 24576) - …` (once per 60 s); W `Wi-Fi list scan without a BLE pulse (%s) - it runs beside BLE scanning`; W `router fallback: retry without a BLE pulse (%s) - its connect runs beside BLE scanning`; W `radio policy: SoftAP %s by the Wi-Fi mode but %s by the wifi_manager callbacks - …` and `radio policy: the SoftAP fact agrees with the Wi-Fi mode again`. Gone: `portal priority …`, `Wi-Fi radio hold ON/OFF …`, `Wi-Fi radio hold: 30 s limit …`. `HEALTH_ENGINE: BLE scanning paused/resumed` is gone with the health hold.
- NimBLE: its INFO lines are compiled out (B4), apart from `ogf=…, ocf=…, hci_err=…` for a failed HCI command.

| `main/radio_policy/radio_policy.c` (new) | +10,859 B | +3,658 B | +418 B | +16 B (its two spinlocks) |
| `main/radio_policy/radio_lab.c` (new; empty with the option off) | 0 | 0 | 0 | 0 |

- **`.bss` gate:** about **−1.28 KB** against `520b17a` (phase 1 left −1.73 KB): it holds, with about 1.2 KB of room.
- **I10:** no task, timer, queue, event registration or heap allocation added (the only `xTaskCreate` is `ble_leak_scan`'s, as before). No IRAM, DRAM or RTC section in any object but the old ones (`lora.cpp` 10 B, `reset_button.c` 48 B). **The external symbol set at HEAD is `1057ee1`'s less `uxTaskPriorityGet`** (298 symbols; IDF still links that one from `pthread.c`, `esp_ipc.c`, `idf_additions.c`), so nothing new can land in IRAM: DIRAM `.text` stays 113,387 B. Heap at rest: about 32 B less in use (B4 removed NimBLE's log-tag node); the `ble_phy` namespace entry (about 30 B) stays, the 15s exception.
- **Link check without linking:** across the 32 objects no strong symbol is defined twice and no project symbol collides with a library's; every external reference is defined by the image's libraries (CP5's `build\esp-idf\*.a` less the project's own, IDF's prebuilt Wi-Fi, PHY, coexistence and BT controller libraries, the toolchain's libc, libm, libstdc++ and libgcc, the ROM and SoC linker scripts), except the eight `_binary_*` asset symbols that `components/wifi_portal/CMakeLists.txt` embeds at build time. The lab image adds exactly one external, `esp_wifi_config_11b_rate` (libnet80211, flash).
- **APP_RADIO_LAB=n is untouched by the lab code:** the 31 production objects at HEAD are byte-identical (allocated section contents, relocations, symbol tables) to those of HEAD with `434bda1` … `71398db` reverted (the reverse patch, its context-shifted hunks resolved by hand), and `radio_lab.o` is empty. Warnings: only the four known ones, at their lines, in all three builds.
- **Lab image** (`APP_RADIO_LAB=y` against production, at HEAD): code +3,979 B, `.rodata` +2,185 B, `.bss` +82 B (K1's station table and a flag per request), `.data` +1 B.
- **Stack** (`-fstack-usage` at HEAD): `ble_leak_scan_task` 112 B (208 B in phase 1), `executor_pass` 80 B, `exec_summary` 288 B + `radio_policy_exec_summary` 128 B, `radio_policy_exec_wifi_grant` 112 B, `exec_wifi_pulse` 64 B, `read_whitelist` 416 B, `phy_save` 288 B (the NVS chain below it is still the deepest path); `list_scan_gate` 48 B (wifi_manager task), `ap_stations_prune` 224 B (`wifi_task`), `router_retry` 32 B. T6-11 measures them.

**The reviews and what became of each finding** (six lenses on `1057ee1` … `71398db`; fixer round 1 on top; every fix compiled and model-checked in `…\finish2\fix1\`)

| Finding | Severity | In short | Fate |
|---|---|---|---|
| LEAK-1 = M1 | major | every SUBMIT reset the claim's I2b spacing: Connects about every 5 s withheld a pending leak response's claim, so RMLEAK and CLOSE, indefinitely (0 claims in 900 s, 8 of 8 seeds) | fixed `2f70ad8`: every Wi-Fi kind leaves the claim 2.5 s of room, and while a claim is due no Wi-Fi pulse is granted unless the last pulse was a claim (36-38 claims per 900 s under the attack, 39 with none) |
| LEAK-2 | minor | the starvation guard removed the valve's 1M slot for 110 s per 600 s under a leak response | fixed `1fd5579` (the guard runs AP_K1M then) |
| LEAK-3 | minor | the recovery is Coded only: a known-1M sensor can be 1M-blind for about 5.2 s around a pulse | open: document (plan 5.5 note added tonight); a persistent leak is caught at the next heartbeat |
| LEAK-4 = COEX-4 | nit | a JOIN granted after its end rule gave a pulse of 20 ms or less that reset I2b | fixed `ff0b424`; its last gap (a JOIN pending one or two ticks before its end rule passed) closed by `d6c8b69` (phase 3, 15w) |
| LEAK-5 | nit | the CONNECT pulse's end relies on NimBLE | rejected: NimBLE's master timer cancels at the claim's deadline; the backstop (deadline + 2 s, host reset) now covers every case (`c49bc9c`) |
| LEAK-6 | nit | RETRY and LIST going on beside BLE are unaccounted; a router retry is sent in LR_AP's first 30 s | rejected: plan 4.4's grant protocol and plan 5.3 (rejoin and alert during a leak response); option open: defer only LIST there |
| PH-1 | major | a join assist was refused for good after 2 s whenever any pulse had ended 6-7 s earlier (18 % of S3 joins, 10 % in the portal) | fixed `ff0b424`: a JOIN waits while its station settles (up to 10 s) and is dropped once it left or its end rule passed |
| PH-2 | major | the TAIL (STA's IP, SoftAP up) runs N_CODED while the phone still needs the result, the CSA re-join and Finish; G1 cannot compare a tail setting | open: plan says TAIL runs NORMAL; P-6, P-8, P-9 blocking in S2 at CP7; a bounded tail rule (about 10 lines) if they fail |
| PH-3 | minor | valve claims allowed between a station's join and its lease (up to about 3.7 s with no Wi-Fi) | fixed `1e39cdb` (no claim while a station joins, outside a leak response) |
| PH-4 | minor | I8 broken at row switches (a 1M slot straight into the next row's Coded window) | fixed `0ee0ef9` |
| PH-5 | minor | the list-scan gate asked for a pulse while a STA attempt was in flight, and the scan then failed | fixed `b46f23c` |
| PH-6 | minor | with a 1M or unknown-PHY sensor every AP mode is AP_K1M (33 % Wi-Fi), which G1 does not bench | open: bench plan (S2 runs with such sensors, 15v) |
| PH-7 | nit | a pending JOIN held the "station joining" check true past 2 s | fixed `ff0b424` |
| F1 | minor | the claim-overrun backstop did nothing for a connect this module no longer tracked: BLE off indefinitely (I3) | fixed `c49bc9c` (host reset in every case, one at a time; the executor then polls every 500 ms) |
| F2 | minor | the LR claim's 2.5 s reservation applied to SUBMIT only | fixed `2f70ad8` |
| F3 | nit | a second Connect's SUBMIT could be dropped racing the first's end | fixed `f0bd523` |
| F4 | nit | (a) a retracted grant could be seen as ON; (b) a withdraw racing a grant leaves a stray pulse of about 0 ms | (a) fixed `96e33e0`; (b) accepted (rare, bounded) |
| F5 | nit | a second station's JOIN was dropped, not queued; a waiting SUBMIT held `join_settling()` true | fixed `ff0b424` (owed and asked later) |
| F6 | nit | `pdMS_TO_TICKS` truncation made the advert-interval threshold 540 ms, not 548 | fixed `31e93b9` (56 ticks) |
| COEX-1 | minor | a late executor restarted a full Wi-Fi slot and widened the Coded gap (I1 broken in LR_AP for 300-500 ms stalls) | fixed `92cb7ec` |
| COEX-2 | minor | SUBMIT and JOIN waited up to 548 ms for a young Coded scan | fixed `e85b7e4` (granted into it with ≥ 1.5 s of budget) |
| COEX-3 | minor | a non-LR claim in AP_IDLE held the RF up to 2.5 s (4.3 s with the recovery) | AP_IDLE part fixed `1e39cdb` (exactly 1.5 s, none while a station joins); NORMAL's 1.5-2.5 s open |
| COEX-5 | minor | production list scans run with `coex_background_scan` off; the home-channel return under coexistence may need it | open: bench (time SCAN_DONE − start; beacons during the scan) |
| MB-1 … MB-4 | docs | CP7's line count and hash, size bands, the NimBLE INFO exception, the row count | fixed `581e1a0`; MB-4's bench line corrected here (14 rows) |
| MB-5 | hardening | nothing asserted that every row but RECOVERY carries `RP_F_I1` | fixed `52ef6a2` (static assert and self-test; mutation-tested) |
| M2 | model | B2's hunt never backed off: NORMAL with a dead valve lost its guarantee (TAIL 31 s against 16.5 s) | fixed `d7a918e` |
| M3 | model | B2's own gate is predicted to fail; the stated cause was wrong | geometry fixed `96b09c0` (0.45 s); the gate's wording open |
| M4 | model | a sniffer-aligned Connect flood fits I2b and pushes SERVE-A past 60 s | closed in the model in phase 3 (`509e6b6`, then `4e8a8cd`: SUBMIT and JOIN paced, one stamp; 15w); ratify (15x) |
| M5 | model | B3's window counts from boot or provisioning | open: documented tonight; optional code after G0 |
| M6 | nit | the dither's dead time: N_CODED p99 4.0 s at p_loss 0.5 (plan 3.5), NORMAL_LR p99.9 60.5 s (plan 47.5) | open: documented tonight (plan note) |

**The council** (on `1057ee1` … `581e1a0`): **5/5 SHIP, no blocking issue** (leak safety, phones, RTOS, coexistence, memory and build). Each member re-read its area at HEAD; the leak, coexistence and memory members also compiled HEAD in scratch (only the four known warnings). Verified, among others: RMLEAK-before-CLOSE and `rules_engine.c` untouched; a claim due under a leak response always reaches a Coded window end with 2.5 s of budget, so the claim-first rule cannot deadlock with Wi-Fi; I5 (only the executor calls `ble_gap_ext_disc` / `ble_gap_disc_cancel`, the only `ble_gap_connect` is through `exec_claim()`); no nested spinlock, no logging or blocking inside one, no lost wake, no busy loop; the host reset is bounded (`ble_hs_sched_reset` → `ble_gap_reset_state` → `ble_gap_master_failed`); B4 moves no IRAM; the SoftAP fact has one writer and cannot be missed. Their non-blocking items are in the residuals and decisions below.

**Model checks** (no G0 data, so **the council's models ran with the plan's assumptions on the implemented parameters**: Ta U(312.5, 437.5) ms + 0-10 ms, p_loss 0.3 / 0.5, cadence 15 s, bursts 2.5 s / edge 4.0 s, n = 20,000; scripts and outputs under `…\finish2\review_model\`, `…\finish2\fix1\model\`, `…\finish2\wifi\model\`, `…\finish2\lab\model\`). **The re-run with G0's measured Ta and p_loss that WP8 owes is still to do, after G0.**
- **Invariants, shadow model** (`fix1\model\shadow_fix.py`, a transcription of the executor and `radio_policy` at HEAD with the real call pattern: portal, fallback, tail and normal sessions, joins, leases, probes, page loads, LIST at the page trigger, Rescans, Connects, RETRY every 30-35 s, claims, leak responses): 100 seeds of 30 min, **0 findings** for I1, I2 (counting only Coded windows of 548 ms or more), the recovery, I2b, I8 (BLE runs closer than 250 ms counted as one) and I7; requester waits ≤ 2.0 s; join-assist end lateness 0 ms. 40 seeds under a Connect flood: 0 findings. With each fix switched off alone, its findings come back (I8 runs 136 in 60 seeds, I2 86).
- **Leak guarantees as built**, p99 at p_loss 0.3 / 0.5 [plan]: AP_IDLE 2.5 / 4.0 [3.0 / 15.5]; SERVE-A 3.5 / 16.5 [4.0 / 17.0]; SERVE-A + LIST 4.0 / 17.0 [15.5 / 17.5]; SERVE-A + a Rescan every 20-25 s 4.5 / 17.5 [15.5 / 30.5]; SUBMIT at the edge 15.5 / 30.5 [17 / 31.5]; JOIN at the edge 15.5 / 17.0; LR_AP 16.0 / 31.5 [16 / 32]; N_MIXED (B3) 3.5 / 16.5 [3.5 / 16.5]; TAIL 16.5 [16.5]; NORMAL with B2's claims 3.5 / 15.5 [3.0 / 15.5]; N_CODED 2.5 / 4.0 [2.5 / 3.5] (M6). The portal bound (p99 ≤ 16 / 32 s) holds, AP_K1M included. Short 1 s wettings: AP_IDLE .882 / .730, SERVE-A .811 / .636, N_CODED .903 / .770 [.92 / .79].
- **Persistent leaks:** NORMAL's de-lock at the worst Ta detects within 60 s with probability 1.0000 for N_CODED and N_HUNT; the reset portal in AP_IDLE p99.9 4 s / 17.5 s, within 60 s 1.0000; every G1 rung within 60 s 1.0000 at p_loss 0.3 and ≥ 0.998 at 0.5.
- **Valve relink** (B2, above) and **in the AP modes without a leak response** (`wifi\model\refind_ap.py`): AP_IDLE p50 / p90 4.0 / 13.4 s at valve p_loss 0.1 and 5.9 / 19.9 s at 0.3; SERVE-A 6.0 / 17.5 s and 8.6 / 26.7 s (claims also wait while a page is used); LR_AP and NORMAL's N_HUNT about 2 s p50. G6 (linked ≤ 5 s): over 5 s in 3.5 % / 16 % / 39 % of cycles in LR_AP after its first 30 s, 0.3 % / 5.4 % / 22 % in NORMAL_LR, at valve p_loss 0.1 / 0.3 / 0.5.
- **Attacks:** LEAK-1's Connect-and-Disconnect every 3-5.8 s now leaves 36-38 claims per 900 s (0 before the fix). A Connect flood at I2b's ceiling gives the other sensors p99.9 61.5 s / 77 s at p_loss 0.3 / 0.5, and a sniffer-aligned one 62 s / 137 s in SERVE-A (M4); I1, I2, I2b and claim-first hold throughout.
- **List scans:** about 43 % of list scans run without a LIST pulse (mostly I2b's spacing after the page's own join assist), and some are refused by I7 while the phone's own assist tail runs, then ordered again 10 s later (after the page's 8 s "No networks found").

| Constant (file) | Value | Fixed by |
|---|---|---|
| `RP_ADV_SMAX_MS` (`radio_policy.h`) | 448 ms (FW 1.1.0: Ta ≤ 437.5 ms + 10 ms) | **G0**: the burst lines' shortest dT per sensor; a longer Ta makes the asserts name the row that breaks I1 |
| `LIST_DMA_MIN_FREE` (`app_wifi.c`) | 24 KB of internal DMA heap at a list scan | **G0** (plan 4.4) |

| `RP_SERVE_RUNG` (`radio_policy.h`) | SERVE-A [C 0.6][W 0.6] (SERVE-A-thin and SERVE-B selectable) | **G1** (15v) |
| the APIDLE row | [C 0.6][W 0.3] (W 0.6 in the lab) | **G1** |
| `RP_DISC_EVERY_AP` / `_SERVE` (backed off `_BO`) | every 2nd / 3rd period (6th / 7th) | **G1** |
| `RP_JOIN_LEASE_TAIL_MS` / `RP_JOIN_PROBE_TAIL_MS` | 1.5 s / 0.3 s (the assist's end rule) | **G1** |
| `RP_JOIN_SPACING_MS` + `_JIT_MS`; `RP_JOIN_SETTLE_MS` | 30 s + U(0, 10 s); 10 s (I7, and how long a JOIN waits) | **G1**, G5 |
| `RP_PAGE_ACTIVE_MS`; `RP_PROV_SERVE_MS` / `_EVERY_MS`; `RP_HOT_MS` | 60 s; 60 s once per station per 10 min; 10 s (SERVE-A-thin) | **G1** |
| `RP_UNALIGNED_MIN_MS` | 1.5 s (a SUBMIT or JOIN into a young Coded scan) | **G1** |
| `RP_I8_WIFI_MIN_MS` (lead slot) | 0.3 s | G1 |
| K1 (`RP_LAB_K1_MS`, `RP_LAB_K1_EVERY_MS`) | 1.5 s, once per station per 60 s; lab only | **G1** |

| `CLAIM_EMPTY_FREE`; `VALVE_HEARD_HOLD_MS` (`app_ble_valve.c`) | 1 (the back-off from the 2nd empty claim); 7 s | the 100 cycles, G6b |
| `RP_CONNECT_MS` / `RP_CONNECT_LR_MS` | 1.5 s (AP_IDLE exactly) to 2.5 s (NORMAL, clipped); 2.5 s under a leak response | the 100 cycles, G6 (decision above) |
| `CLAIM_OVERRUN_MS` (`app_ble_leak.c`) | 2 s past the pulse, then a host reset | should never fire |
| `RP_UNKNOWN_PHY_MS` (B3) | 600 s | G2 with a 1M sensor |
| starvation guard `STARVE_MS` / `RP_STARVE_FOR_MS` / `RP_STARVE_EVERY_MS` | 250 s / 110 s / 600 s (plan) | G4 |
| I2 / I2b: `RP_BLIND_MAX_MS`, `RP_RECOVERY_MS`, `RP_I2B_SPACING_MS` + `RP_I2B_JITTER_MS`, `RP_I2B_BLIND_MAX_MS` | 2.8 s, 1.2 s, 6 s + U(0, 1 s), 12 s per 60 s (16 × 4 s buckets) | plan values; G2, G5 |
| `RP_LR_OVERLAY_CAP_MS`; `RP_LR_AP_30_MS`; `RP_LR_JOIN_HOLDOFF_MS` / `_EVERY_MS` | 600 s; 30 s; 30 s / 60 s | plan values; G6 |
| `RP_RETRY_MS`; `ROUTER_RETRY_MS` + `_JITTER_MS`; `ROUTER_RETRY_JOIN_MAX_MS`; `ROUTER_RETRY_PAGE_MAX_MS` | 1.5 s; 30 s + U(0, 5 s); 10 s; 300 s | G7, G8 |
| `RP_GRANT_WAIT_MS`; `RP_STA_PRUNE_MS`; `AP_FACT_PASSES`; `RP_REFUSE_LOG_MS`; `LIST_LOW_LOG_MS` | 2 s; 2 s; 3 passes; 10 s; 60 s | implementers' choices |
| `SUMMARY_PRIO`; `CHORES_MIN_SCAN_MS`; duty watchdog | 4; 1 s; below 80 % in 2 summaries | implementers' choices; T6-11, G4 |

**For the user's decision** (each with the recommendation of the fixer and the council)
1. **Ratify B1-B5 and the SUBMIT rule** (the table above).
2. **B2's gate:** restate "100 power cycles relink ≤ 10 s" statistically (e.g. at most 2 of 100 over 10 s), record the valve's p_loss (adverts heard per 1M slot), and let the bench choose 0.3 or 0.45 s; G6's "linked ≤ 5 s" likewise. *Recommended.*
3. **The NORMAL claim length** (1.5-2.5 s; the plan says 1.5 s): keep for CP7, decide on the bench numbers. *Recommended.*
4. **The tail (PH-2):** keep the plan's "TAIL runs NORMAL" for CP7 with P-6, P-8 and P-9 blocking in S2; if they fail, approve a bounded tail rule (SERVE rows while the SoftAP is up, the STA connected and the page used in the last 60 s, at most the tail's 60-75 s; about 10 lines). The phone member adds a 2-line option: defer non-LR claims while the SoftAP is up with a station on it. *Recommended: decide after S2.*
5. **The Connect flood (M4):** before release, a SUBMIT within 30 s of another takes the 6 s spacing (or a per-client Connect limit). *Recommended.* *(Phase 3, 15w: built as a 45 s window with one stamp for SUBMIT and JOIN, `4e8a8cd`; for ratification, 15x.)*
6. **A claim delayed by pulses made before a leak response** (council, leak member): the 2.5 s reservation applies only once the trigger is set, so if pulses already used more than 9.5 s of the window, the first claim waits for buckets to roll out (up to about 60 s worst case; RMLEAK/CLOSE delayed, never lost). Options after the bench: reserve the claim's room whenever the valve is wanted and unlinked, or exempt an episode's first claim from the room check (still inside I2 and its recovery).
7. **List scans without a pulse:** exempt the first LIST after a station's lease from the spacing (as SUBMIT is), and let the gate wait out the requesting phone's own assist tail (at most 1.5 s) instead of refusing. *Decide with G1's list-completeness data.*
8. **LEAK-3 and M5:** documented in plan 5.5 tonight; M5's optional code after G0 if installers can power a 1M sensor 10 min or more after provisioning.
9. **The B1 interplay:** with a valve that is heard but never links, claims use nearly all of I2b's room under a leak response; about 1/3 of Connects get a SUBMIT pulse, with grant latency p50 3.5 s (the Connect still runs, beside BLE). *Recommended: accept.*
10. **A dead valve with the incident latched** hunts at the full rate until LEAK_RESET (other sensors p99 3.5 / 16 s, p99.9 about 31 s, under 60 s). *Recommended: accept.*
11. **LEAK-6's option:** defer only LIST (not RETRY) in LR_AP's first 30 s, if a valve-only radio is preferred over the page's list there.
12. **MB-3:** keep `CONFIG_BT_NIMBLE_PRINT_ERR_NAME=y`. *Recommended.*
13. **Pre-push cleanup** (in the rebase already planned for `6b84ae3`'s password and `9583236`'s subject): reword `96b09c0`'s type `tune` to `fix` or `perf`; `13ae5b2`'s body says `esp_wifi_ap_get_sta_list` is newly referenced, but `ap_station_count()` already called it; 15r's six over-long WP4 subjects and three body figures.

**Residual risks** (none blocks CP7; the owner in brackets)
1. **No bench data behind any radio constant** (G0, G1): everything above is provisional, and the model re-run with measured Ta and p_loss is owed. [G0, then the model; G1]
2. **First hardware run of the whole radio policy:** the timed rows, the pulses, the grant protocol and the host-reset backstop have never run on a hub; S2 joins in AP_IDLE (33 % Wi-Fi; association precedes any assist) are the main phone risk, with no run-time fallback now that the window is gone. The lab image's knobs (15v) are the first lever. [CP7, G-CNA S2, G1]
3. **The claim path still depends on the controller's LE Scan Timeout** (15s residual 1). [CP7 VAL-01]
4. **Post-cap AP modes:** a pended CLOSE in the portal more than 10 min into an incident waits for a slow relink (p50 4-9 s, p90 13-27 s); never dropped. [field note]
5. **Open SoftAP:** a stranger can flood Connects (M4; closed in the model in phase 3, 15w), keep joining with new MACs (defers non-LR claims and list scans 10 s per join, I7), or make the hub forget its Wi-Fi (D9 kept); leak protection keeps running in each case. [decision 5; WPA2 excluded by the user]
6. **Coexistence unknowns:** list scans with `coex_background_scan` off (COEX-5); the claim's 1M initiator at 100 % duty with Coded RX not time-limited (up to 1.5 s + 1.2 s with no Wi-Fi slot in AP_IDLE, up to 2.5 s + 1.2 s beside the STA in NORMAL); `csa_count` 3 beacons falling in Coded windows. [G0, G1, G-CNA P-8]
7. **RTOS nits** (council): a recovery owed across a host reset leaves RETRY and LIST unanswered until resync (they go on after 2 s); the grant step's settle loop is not compare-and-set (one scan or retry beside BLE at worst); an owed JOIN does not kick the executor (picked up within 500 ms); an extra assist is dropped if another JOIN is busy; `last_pulse` is not reset between leak-response episodes (a rare Connect in LR_AP's first 30 s can delay the first claim about 8-11 s, the coexistence member's fix is one line). [later hardening]
8. **`list_scan_gate()` holds the wifi_manager task up to 2 s**, so its queue waits meanwhile. [S2, S3: watch for `queue full` lines]
9. **`[SUMMARY]` at priority 4** can delay the next slot under heavy CPU load (TLS handshakes). [watch `longest Coded gap` around cloud reconnects]
10. **The lab image's NVS namespace `rp_lab`** stays after reflashing production (about 30 B of heap, bench hubs only). [none]

**What to watch at CP7 and on the bench** (15t has the boot; these supersede the stages' own lists, with the review's corrections)
- **The 100 valve power cycles:** `[DISCONNECT] reason=0x08` → `Scan mode N_CODED with a valve hunt (valve not linked): … then 0.45 s on 1M …` → `[SCAN] Target MAC matched` → `[CLAIM] Connecting to the valve: pulse up to NNNN ms` (1500-2500; exactly 1500 in AP_IDLE; 2500 with `(leak response pending)`) → `GAP CONNECT EVENT` status 0 → `SETUP COMPLETE`. Time power-on → `Target MAC matched` and → linked; the model expects p50 about 1.6-2.1 s and about 0.07-1.7 % of cycles over 10 s at valve p_loss 0.1-0.3, nearly all a late first hearing. Empty claims: the first prints `[CLAIM] Valve claim failed (…), 1 in a row - the next needs only the pulse spacing, no back-off`, the second `… 2 in a row - next claim in 10 s …`. `RADIO: [CLAIM] Valve claim waits for the pulse-rate limit (I2b): …` after a pulse is normal. After 10 min unlinked: `Scan mode N_CODED with a valve hunt, backed off (valve unlinked 10 min or more): … every 6 periods 0.45 s more on 1M …` and `RADIO: Discovery backed off: …`.
- **P11 with the valve unpowered:** `[LR] Leak response pending, valve not linked (RMLEAK/CLOSE pended) - leak-response scanning, at most 600 s for this incident`, W `RADIO: Mode NORMAL_LR (a leak response, the valve not linked)` and `Scan mode NORMAL_LR …`; exactly 600 s later `[LR] Leak-response scanning ended after 600 s (…)`, `RADIO: Mode NORMAL …` and the hunt's scan mode (not backed off while the incident is latched). Power the valve: a 2500 ms claim, then `[CMD] Applying pending RMLEAK command=1` **before** `[CMD] Applying pending valve command=0` (G6: linked ≤ 5 s, judged statistically).
- **D2, the reset portal with sensors provisioned:** `SoftAP up with no saved Wi-Fi credentials (setup portal) - BLE scanning, if any, stays on beside it`, `RADIO: Mode AP_IDLE …`; every live sensor's advert count non-zero within a few minutes; AP_IDLE or SERVE seconds in `[SUMMARY]`; a BLE sensor never `device_offline` in a portal session longer than 10 min; an unlinked valve goes CRITICAL after 180 s as in normal operation (it is hunted, no hold). While any listed sensor's PHY is unknown (never heard since provisioning), every boot, and so every S2 run, runs AP_K1M for up to 10 min (B3, PH-6), and a sensor known on 1M gives AP_K1M always (no line of its own; `[SUMMARY]` shows each sensor's `C`, `M` or `?`): record `PHY table loaded: N of N sensor(s) known, M on 1M` before each S2 run.
- **A phone joins (S2, S3):** `SoftAP: station <MAC> joined, AID=n`, `RADIO: JOIN pulse for station <MAC>: BLE off for up to N ms` (N ≤ 2800; often 2.0-2.5 s; since phase 3, 15w, a join within 45 s of the last SUBMIT or JOIN pulse is paced: `… up to 1500 ms (paced: a SUBMIT or JOIN pulse began less than 45 s before)`), the lease line, `JOIN pulse over after N ms (its lease and first page or 302 | the station left | its deadline)`, `Mode SERVE (…)`. A second phone now gets its own JOIN pulse after the first's (owed), not `JOIN pulse not granted (not granted within 10 s)` every time. Record join → lease (P-2) and the sign-in auto-open (P-4).
- **The first page load:** `RADIO: LIST pulse: BLE off for up to 2500 ms` and `LIST pulse over after ~2000 ms (its requester ended it)` (COEX-5 may change the 2000 ms), or about half the time the W line `Wi-Fi list scan without a BLE pulse (not granted | no answer in 2 s) - it runs beside BLE scanning`; record list completeness (P-5, G7) separately for each. `Wi-Fi list scan not started: a station is joining the SoftAP …` with a second phone, or right after the phone's own join; `… a connect attempt is in flight …` during a Connect or a router retry.
- **A Connect:** `Wi-Fi setup page: Connect - attempt started`, `RADIO: SUBMIT pulse: BLE off for up to N ms` (N ≤ 2800; since phase 3, 15w, usually paced, because the phone's own JOIN pulse began less than 45 s before: `SUBMIT pulse waits for the pulse spacing (x of y s): paced, …`, then `SUBMIT pulse: BLE off for up to 1500 ms (paced: a SUBMIT or JOIN pulse began less than 45 s before)`), `SUBMIT pulse over after N ms (the Connect's outcome | its deadline)`, then at the IP `Mode NORMAL (SoftAP down, or STA connected)`; tail and Finish lines as at CP6; time status.json, the CSA follow and Finish in the tail (PH-2).
- **Router outage (S3):** `RADIO: RETRY pulse: BLE off for up to 1500 ms`, `router fallback: retrying the configured network (attempt N)`, `RETRY pulse over after 1500 ms (its deadline)` every 30-35 s; `router fallback: retry deferred - the Wi-Fi setup page is open` while the page is used; `router fallback: retry without a BLE pulse (…)` rare (send the log if it repeats every retry; once after a host reset is expected).
- **A leak in the portal with the valve unpowered (P11 in S2, G6):** W `RADIO: Mode LR_AP …`; for 30 s `JOIN pulse not granted (a leak response's first 30 s) - Wi-Fi goes on beside BLE` and the same for RETRY and LIST (one line per 10 s at most); then `<KIND> pulse waits: the valve's claim goes first (a leak response is pending)` at most once per 10 s, claims and Wi-Fi pulses alternating; a powered valve gives `(leak response pending)` 2500 ms claims, then RMLEAK before CLOSE.
- **Every 60 s:** the two `RADIO: [SUMMARY]` lines (15t), `at most Y s in 60 s` ≤ 12.0, `longest Coded gap` ≤ 2,900 ms with `(0 over 2900 ms)`.
- **Never expected** (send the log): `RADIO: I2: BLE went N ms with no Coded scan`, `Profile self-test FAILED`, `Duty watchdog`, either `[CLAIM] … 2 s past its pulse` line, `radio policy: SoftAP up/down by the Wi-Fi mode but …`, a repeating `Failed to start ext scan`, a stack-canary panic on `ble_leak_scan`, `wifi_manager`, `wifi_task`, `httpd` or `sys_evt`; `Wi-Fi list scan not started: internal DMA free N B (needs 24576)` in a single-phone setup means `LIST_DMA_MIN_FREE` is wrong.
- **T6-11:** `ble_leak_scan` (3,072 B) after a JOIN or SUBMIT pulse, a claim, a `PHY table saved` and a `[SUMMARY]`; `wifi_manager` (`list_scan_gate`), `wifi_task` (`ap_stations_prune` 224 B), the default event loop (2,304 B), `httpd`, the NimBLE host.

### 15v. G1 on the lab image: the bench procedure (WP7; after CP7) (2026-10-07)

From the lab stage's procedure (`…\scratchpad\finish2\lab\G1_PROCEDURE.md`), brought to HEAD: the fix round changed the JOIN rules (a JOIN waits while its station settles, `ff0b424`; it can go into a young Coded scan, `e85b7e4`), the claims (none while a station joins, exactly 1.5 s in AP_IDLE, `1e39cdb`) and the lab image's sizes. Plan references: 2.4, 4.3, 4.4 (K1), 4.9 (C11), 7.5 (G-CNA's P-1 … P-6, states S1-S3), 11 (WP7), 12 (G1). `MANUAL_TEST_PLAN.md` gets a G1 section from this one in WP10.

**What G1 decides**

| Decision | Knob on the lab image | Production constant it fixes |
|---|---|---|
| The SERVE rung (plan 4.3, D8) | keys `1`-`5` | `RP_SERVE_RUNG` (provisional SERVE-A) |
| AP_IDLE density: C0.6/W0.3 or C0.6/W0.6 | key `i` | the APIDLE row (provisional W0.3) |
| The join assist and its end rule | key `j` (assist on/off) | the JOIN pulse (kept or not; end-rule tails 1.5 s / 0.3 s) |
| Contingency K1 (plan 4.4) | key `k` | K1 is built in the lab only; production gets it only if G1 says so |
| C11 (plan 4.9): SoftAP 11b rates; `coex_background_scan` on the list scan | keys `b`, `c` | data only (none in production); `c` also answers COEX-5 (15u) |
| TAIL under N_CODED; AP_K1M and discovery | none: they follow from the facts (6.6 below) | – |

G1 also measures, per phone, join → first DNS in S2 (plan 2.4), and re-checks page p90 at the httpd socket cap that G-CNA S1 fixed.

**Rule (plan 4.3).** Ship the rung with the **highest Coded share** that passes, in state S2, on all four phone classes, 10 runs each. Ties go to the better short-wetting figure (A beats C). SERVE-C ships only if the user accepts an I8 exemption for associated stations; otherwise it is data only. If no rung passes, plan 11's fallback applies.

**1. Build the lab image** (PowerShell, IDF 5.5.1 environment, project folder). The lab image is the production firmware plus Kconfig `APP_RADIO_LAB=y`. Build it **after CP7**, in a build folder and with an `sdkconfig` of its own, so the project's `sdkconfig` and `build\` are never touched (a `y` in the project's `sdkconfig` would stay for every later build). Without the option every production object is the same byte for byte (checked on all 31 objects at `71398db` and again at HEAD, 15u); a lab image also needs `APP_BENCH_DIAG` (on in 2.1.4 development builds), and the build stops otherwise.

```powershell
git log --oneline -1                 # b651701 or a later docs-only commit (CP7, 15t)
Get-FileHash sdkconfig               # CP7's: 9E13270C...AAE0412D (15t)
$lab = "$env:TEMP\build_lab"
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force $lab | Out-Null
(Get-Content sdkconfig) | Where-Object { $_ -notmatch '^(# )?CONFIG_APP_RADIO_LAB[ =]' } | Set-Content "$lab\sdkconfig.lab"
Add-Content "$lab\sdkconfig.lab" "CONFIG_APP_RADIO_LAB=y"
idf.py -B $lab -D SDKCONFIG="$lab\sdkconfig.lab" build *> "$env:TEMP\build_lab.log" ; "exit=$LASTEXITCODE"
Select-String -Path "$env:TEMP\build_lab.log" -Pattern 'warning:|error:' | ForEach-Object Line
Compare-Object (Get-Content sdkconfig) (Get-Content "$lab\sdkconfig.lab")
Get-FileHash "$lab\sdkconfig.lab"
idf.py -B $lab -D SDKCONFIG="$lab\sdkconfig.lab" size
Get-FileHash sdkconfig               # unchanged
git status --short                   # unchanged
```

Expected:
- `exit=0`; the same four known warnings as CP7 (`app_ble_valve.c:106:9`, `app_lora.cpp:194:5` ×2, `app_lora.cpp:160:13`; the lab's hook in `app_lora.cpp` sits below them on purpose), nothing else.
- `Compare-Object` prints exactly one pair: `=> CONFIG_APP_RADIO_LAB=y`, `<= # CONFIG_APP_RADIO_LAB is not set`. The lab `sdkconfig`'s SHA256, from CP7's file: **`0A3704F0A13553E231AE92C5A2F760794C2F1406EA3B4BCAE3954F63EDB3981C`** (kconfgen in scratch, by the lab stage and again by the docs owner at HEAD; record it either way). Its `sdkconfig.h` differs from CP7's by one line, `#define CONFIG_APP_RADIO_LAB 1`.
- An `#error "APP_RADIO_LAB is a bench image: it needs APP_BENCH_DIAG …"` means `APP_BENCH_DIAG` is off: this is not a 2.1.4 development tree.
- Sizes against CP7 (objects at `b651701`): code about +4.0 KB (+3,954 B; +3,979 B at `52ef6a2`), `.rodata` +2,185 B, `.bss` +82 B, `.data` +1 B; newly linked: `esp_wifi_config_11b_rate` (flash). **DIRAM `.text` stays 113,387 B** (no IRAM in the lab code).
- The project's `sdkconfig` hash and `git status` are as before.

Flash and monitor (keys are typed into the monitor): `idf.py -B $lab -D SDKCONFIG="$lab\sdkconfig.lab" -p COM5 flash monitor --timestamps`. Save each step's log: in the monitor, `Ctrl+T` then `Ctrl+L` starts and stops a log file; name it per ladder step (for example `g1_step3_serveA_iphone.txt`).

**Back to production after G1:** `idf.py -p COM5 flash` from the project's own build (CP7's `build\`), then check that the boot has no `RADIO_LAB:` line. The lab's NVS namespace `rp_lab` (default partition, one small blob) stays; production never reads it. Press `x` before reflashing if the hub will run the lab image again.

**2. The lab image's boot**, in addition to CP7's VAL-01 lines (15t):
- `W RADIO_LAB: radio lab image (APP_RADIO_LAB, the G1 ladder) - not for release`;
- `I RADIO_LAB: [LAB] settings at boot - SERVE rung SERVE-A; AP_IDLE Coded 0.6 s / Wi-Fi 0.3 s; join assist on; K1 off; SoftAP 11b rates on; list-scan coex_background_scan off` (the defaults are the production values; after keys, the saved ones);
- `I RADIO_LAB: [LAB] keys: 1 AP_IDLE density, 2 SERVE-A-thin, 3 SERVE-A, 4 SERVE-C, 5 SERVE-B; …`;
- `I RADIO: Profile self-test passed: 16 rows hold I1 and I2, all but the lab's two SERVE-C rows I8 and the period rule (SERVE rung SERVE-A, the lab's)`. **Never** `Profile self-test FAILED`: that pins NORMAL and the ladder means nothing;
- with `b` saved: `W wifi_manager: radio lab: SoftAP 11b rates off (C11) - not for release`, or `… not turned off (<error>) - they stay on` (record it; then the `b` data is void);
- every 60 s after the two `[SUMMARY]` lines: `I RADIO_LAB: [LAB] 60 s - <the settings>; K1 off (0 asked)`, so every minute of a log names its ladder step.

**3. The keys** (typed in the monitor; one character, no Enter)

| Key | Effect | Applies |
|---|---|---|
| `1` | SERVE rung **AP_IDLE density**: no SERVE mode at all (D8 (c)); AP_IDLE runs while a page is in use, and valve claims are not deferred | at once: the mode on the next executor pass, the row at the next slot boundary |
| `2` | **SERVE-A-thin**: [C 0.6][W 0.6] for 10 s after a page, a user request or a 302, AP_IDLE's rows otherwise | same |
| `3` | **SERVE-A** (production, provisional): [C 0.6][W 0.6], discovery [C 0.6][W 0.3][M 0.3][W 0.3] every 3rd period | same |
| `4` | **SERVE-C** (lab only): [C 1.0][W 1.0], discovery [C 1.0][W 0.4][M 0.3][W 0.3] every 3rd period. Breaks I8 (1.0 s BLE runs) and the period rule (2.0 s divides 8 s and 100 s); I1 and I2 hold | same |
| `5` | **SERVE-B** (the council's): [C 0.6][W 1.2], discovery [C 0.6][W 0.6][M 0.3][W 0.3] | same |
| `i` | AP_IDLE's Wi-Fi slot 0.3 s ↔ 0.6 s (0.6: [C 0.6][W 0.6]; discovery row unchanged, every 2nd period, every 7th once backed off) | at the next slot boundary |
| `j` | join assist on ↔ off (off: a join asks for no JOIN pulse; I7's 10 s settle still holds RETRY, LIST and non-LR claims) | the next join |
| `k` | K1 on ↔ off (below) | the next DNS query |
| `b` | SoftAP 11b rates on ↔ off (`esp_wifi_config_11b_rate(WIFI_IF_AP, true)`) | **saved; from the next boot** (the settings line says `(off from the next boot)` until then) |
| `c` | `coex_background_scan` on the hub's network-list scan off ↔ on | the next list scan |
| `l` | print the settings line | – |
| `x` | all back to the production values (11b from the next boot) | as above |
| `h` / `?` | print the key list | – |

Every change prints `[LAB] key <k>: … - <all settings>` (and for a rung, `[LAB] SERVE rung <name>: <geometry>`) and is saved in NVS: it survives a reboot and the 10 s reset. `s`, `r`, `d`, `a` stay LoRa's bench keys (`s` transmits).

**K1 as built (lab only).** A leased station's first DNS query before any 302 or page asks for a JOIN pulse of at most 1.5 s, once per station per 60 s. It is a JOIN in all else: within I2 and I2b (so not within the 6 s + U(0, 1) s of profile time after any other pulse; since phase 3, 15w, paced like any JOIN within 45 s of the last SUBMIT or JOIN pulse: 1.5 s from a Coded window's end), held off in a leak response's first 30 s and to one per 60 s after, asked for up to 10 s while it waits (`RP_JOIN_SETTLE_MS`), granted into a young Coded scan when 1.5 s of budget is left, ended by the station's first 302 or page + 0.3 s, and counted as that station's assist (the next join assist for it waits 30-40 s). A K1 still pending when its station gets its 302 or page is dropped. Its line: `I RADIO: [LAB] K1: 10.10.0.x's first DNS query before a 302 or a page - a JOIN pulse of at most 1500 ms asked`, then the usual `JOIN pulse for station …: BLE off for up to <=1500 ms` and `JOIN pulse over after N ms (…)`. Right after a join assist K1 cannot run for about 7 s (I2b's spacing), so with the assist on K1 mostly shows when the assist was clipped long before the first DNS; with the assist off it is the only assist.

**4. Bench setup**
- **Hub:** the lab image. 2-3 BLE leak sensors provisioned, **every PHY known**: the boot shows `PHY table loaded: N of N sensor(s) known` (an unknown PHY runs AP_K1M for 10 min after every boot, B3, which in S2 is every run and confounds the rung comparison; let each sensor be heard once, then reboot). The valve **unpowered first** (discovery periods for its hunt), then **linked** (plan 12).
- **Router** 2.4 GHz, on a channel other than 11 (P-8's channel switch happens at Submit).
- **Phones (mobile data on):** an iPhone (iOS 18/26, Private Address Rotating), a Pixel (Android 15/16, Private DNS Automatic), a Samsung (One UI 6/7), an Android 10-11 phone. Optional: a Windows 11 laptop.
- **Ping at 5/s to 10.10.0.1 from the phone** while the page is open: Android `ping -i 0.2 10.10.0.1` (Termux) or a ping app with a 200 ms interval; iOS a ping app with a 0.2 s interval. Record the loss %.
- **Timing:** a stopwatch, or a screen recording of the phone next to the monitor (its `--timestamps` host clock); the hub's lines carry milliseconds since boot.
- **State S2** (plan 7.5): a 10 s reset with the sensors provisioned. The hub erases the Wi-Fi credentials and reboots into the setup portal: `SoftAP up with no saved Wi-Fi credentials (setup portal) - BLE scanning, if any, stays on beside it`, then `RADIO: Mode AP_IDLE (…) (lab)`. The lab settings are kept through it.

**5. The ladder.** A **smoke pass first** (3 runs per phone per step) to drop clear failures, then the full 10.

*One run (P-1 to P-6 of plan 7.5):*
1. The hub in S2, no phone on its SoftAP for at least 60 s (AP_IDLE, no provisional SERVE left).
2. On the phone: Settings, Wi-Fi, tap `WiFi-Hub-<id>`. Start the stopwatch at the tap.
3. Record: joined (s), any "Unable to join"; the sign-in window opening by itself (s after the join); the page rendered (s after the sign-in opened); the network list non-empty (s), or the empty state at 8 s.
4. Start the ping (5/s) for at least 60 s with the page open; record the loss %.
5. Choose the router, enter the password, Connect: the result (s), then Finish.
6. On the phone: **forget the network** (no auto-join next run). On the hub: the 10 s reset (back to S2).

*Step order:*

| Step | Keys (from `x`) | Measures |
|---|---|---|
| 0 | `x` | baseline: production values (SERVE-A, AP_IDLE W0.3, assist on, K1 off) |
| 1-5 | `1`, `2`, `3`, `4`, `5` in turn, each with `x`'s other values | the rung ladder (the rule above) |
| 6 | the chosen rung, `i` | AP_IDLE W0.6 against W0.3 (tap → join, join → lease, "Unable to join") |
| 7 | the chosen rung and AP_IDLE, `j` | the join assist off against on |
| 8 | only if Android sign-in > 10 s p90 in S2 at the chosen rung: `k`, then `k` with `j` (assist off) | K1 |
| 9 | the chosen settings, `c` | `coex_background_scan`: list completeness (APs above −80 dBm listed), list time (SCAN_DONE − start), the LIST pulse lines; COEX-5 |
| 10 | the chosen settings, `b`, then a reboot (the 10 s reset is one) | 11b rates off: tap → join, ping loss, page; verify the W line and, if possible, in a capture that beacons go at 6 Mbps (OFDM), not 1 Mbps |

Run steps 1-5 with the valve unpowered, then repeat the chosen rung and its neighbours with the valve linked. **Add** (PH-6, 15u): one step's runs with a sensor known on 1M, and with one whose PHY is unknown (AP_K1M), judged against the same thresholds.

*What to record per run.* Phone side as above. Hub side (UART):

| Line | Gives |
|---|---|
| `APP_WIFI: SoftAP: station <MAC> joined, AID=n` | the join (its time) |
| `APP_WIFI: SoftAP: station <MAC> got 10.10.0.x, N ms after joining` | **join → lease** |
| `RADIO: JOIN pulse for station <MAC>: BLE off for up to N ms` / `RADIO: JOIN pulse over after N ms (<why>)` | the assist and its end (`its lease and first page or 302`, `its deadline`, `the station left`) |
| `RADIO: JOIN pulse not granted (<why>) - Wi-Fi goes on beside BLE` | an assist refused (`not granted within 10 s`, a leak response) |
| `APP_WIFI: portal client 10.10.0.x: first DNS query, N ms after joining` | **join → first DNS** (plan 2.4, per phone) |
| `APP_WIFI: portal client 10.10.0.x: first captive probe (302 sent), N ms after joining` / `first page request, N ms` | the probe and the page |
| `RADIO: Mode SERVE (…)` / `Mode AP_IDLE (…)` (with `(lab)` and the rung) | the mode changes |
| `RADIO: [LAB] K1: …` | K1 asked (step 8) |
| `APP_WIFI: Wi-Fi list scan …`, `RADIO: LIST pulse …` | the list's scan, granted or beside BLE |
| `RADIO: [SUMMARY] …` (two lines) and `RADIO_LAB: [LAB] 60 s - …` | modes, BLE scanning %, pulses, blind time, longest Coded gap, joins and join → lease max; the step's settings and K1 count |
| `APP_WIFI: Connected! IP: …`, `SoftAP tail …`, `Wi-Fi setup page: Finish …` | the result and the TAIL (NORMAL, N_CODED) |

A CSV per step: step, rung, AP_IDLE slot, assist, K1, coex, 11b, valve (unpowered/linked), phone, run, tap → join s, unable-to-join, join → lease ms, join → first DNS ms, → 302 ms, → page ms, sign-in open s, page s, list s, result s, ping loss %, JOIN pulse ms and why, K1 asked, notes.

*Pass thresholds per step (plan 4.3 and 7.5), per phone class, 10 runs:*
- tap → join ≤ 5 s p95, with **0** "Unable to join"; join → lease ≤ 3 s p95;
- the sign-in window opens by itself: iPhone ≤ 8 s after the join; Pixel and Samsung ≤ 10 s; the Android 10-11 notification ≤ 15 s;
- page ≤ 3 s after the sign-in opens; list ≤ 5 s (or the empty state at 8 s); result ≤ 10 s p90;
- ping loss ≤ the rung's BLE duty + 10 points: AP_IDLE density 77 %, SERVE-A-thin 60-77 %, SERVE-A 60 %, SERVE-C 60 %, SERVE-B 43 %.

The rungs' leak figures for the tie rule (the council's model, unchanged, on the rows as built; Ta U(312.5, 437.5) ms, cadence 15 s; `…\finish2\lab\model\lab_rungs.py`; the rows have not changed since):

| Rung (Coded share) | Short wetting detected, 0.3 / 0.6 / 1.0 / 2.0 s, p_loss 0.3 | same, p_loss 0.5 | Persistent leak detected within 10 s, p_loss 0.3 / 0.5 | its p99.9 latency, p_loss 0.3 / 0.5 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| AP_IDLE density, AP_IDLE W0.3 (67 %) | .421 / .731 / .860 / .975 | .301 / .544 / .700 / .894 | .9993 / .9874 | 4.0 / 17.5 s |
| SERVE-A, AP_IDLE W0.6 (50 %) | .316 / .560 / .778 / .932 | .226 / .414 / .597 / .809 | .9939 / .9575 | 16.5 / 32.5 s |
| SERVE-C, lab (50 %) | .329 / .517 / .716 / .948 | .235 / .397 / .568 / .831 | .9966 / .9670 | 16.0 / 32.0 s |
| SERVE-B (33 %) | .211 / .372 / .560 / .831 | .151 / .274 / .419 / .665 | .9684 / .8802 | 31.0 / 61.5 s |

Every rung detects a persistent leak within 60 s with probability 1.0000 at p_loss 0.3 and ≥ 0.998 at 0.5 (plain rows, no pulse). The 1 s column reproduces plan 4.3's (.860/.700, .778/.597, .716/.568, .560/.419), so the rows as built are the plan's rungs.

**6. Leak safety during G1** (stop and send the log if any appears): `RADIO: I2: BLE went N ms with no Coded scan (limit 2800 ms) - send this log`; `[SUMMARY]`'s `longest Coded gap` above 2,900 ms, or `(N over 2900 ms)` with N > 0; `Duty watchdog`, `Profile self-test FAILED`, a repeating `Failed to start ext scan` (SERVE-C's 1.0 s Coded scan and the [W 0.4] slot are new on hardware); a live sensor with no adverts in `[SUMMARY]` for 5 minutes. Optional, once per rung: wet a sensor while a phone loads the page: `eleak <MAC> — leak=1 …` within 35 s (G2's SERVE bound), and with the valve linked RMLEAK then CLOSE as in P11.

**7. The other G1 items**
- **TAIL under N_CODED** (PH-2): after each run's Connect, the SoftAP tail with the phone on it runs NORMAL: record the result page and Finish (P-6, P-9) times in the tail and the CSA follow (P-8); `[SUMMARY]` shows NORMAL time and BLE scanning ≥ 90 %.
- **AP_K1M and discovery:** one step's 10 runs with a sensor whose PHY is unknown (newly provisioned: AP_K1M for 10 min after its provisioning or the boot) or a 1M sensor (AP_K1M throughout), and the valve unpowered (discovery periods): the same thresholds; record that AP_K1M ran (no mode line of its own: the `[SUMMARY]` adverts show `M` for a 1M sensor).
- **T6-11 on the lab image** if it runs long: the executor's grant frame is 16 B larger and the summary chain 96 B deeper (`radio_lab_log()`); the DNS task (3,072 B) gains K1's 80 B and a log call.

**8. After G1.** Record per step the pass/fail table and the rung chosen by the rule, the AP_IDLE slot, whether the assist stays (and its tails), whether K1 is needed, and the C11 data, in plan 4.3's table (a dated note) and here. The production change that follows is the firmware lead's (a WP8 follow-up, then a build checkpoint): `RP_SERVE_RUNG` in `radio_policy.h`; the APIDLE row if W0.6 wins (with its backed-off cadence every 7th period, as the lab's `RP_LAB_DISC_EVERY_AP06_BO`); K1 moved out of `#if CONFIG_APP_RADIO_LAB` only if needed; SERVE-C only with the user's I8 exemption and a period-rule decision. Then reflash production (step 1's last paragraph).

### 15w. Phase 3: M4 and the JOIN paced, WP9 staged, the whole-branch review and its fixes, a 3/3 council; CP7 moves to `b651701`; WP10 (2026-10-07)

**Why and how.** The same instruction of 2026-10-06 ("Finish everything first and then we'll test tomorrow the build and then work on the issues. Remember to run adversarial workflows on the work areas that you are working on", and "continue with top quality in mind"). An orchestrator ran these stages in one working tree, each on its own files and in its own scratch folder (`…\scratchpad\finish3\`): **rp_harden** (15u's open M4: the Connect flood), then **two adversarial reviews of it** (a leak-model review, `rev_leak_m4\`, and a phone-UX review, `rv_ux_m4\`: both needs_fix, 9 findings between them) and **a fix round** (`m4j\`); **WP9** (plan §8, D14: the memory set staged off by default, `wp9\`) and **its adversarial review** (`wp9_adv\`: needs_fix, one critical); **a whole-branch review** of `fix/2.1.4` (seven lenses: leak safety, crash and wrap-around, concurrency, the cloud contract, upgrade and rollback, build, the portal; `wb_*\`) and **a fixer** for its major finding (`fix_wbcrash\`); **a 3-member council** (leak safety, RTOS, build: `council_leak\`, `council_rtos_p3\`, `council_build\`); the **V5 delta** for the app document (`v5delta\`); and this **docs and checkpoint stage, WP10** (`wp10\`). No hardware: nothing ran `idf.py`, nothing touched `build\`, nothing was flashed; every compile was a scratch compile with `build\compile_commands.json`'s flags against kconfgen-regenerated headers. **No bench data exists for any of it.**

**Commits** (after `d0d2bd3`; every subject ≤ 72 characters with the `Co-Authored-By` line; CRLF kept in the sources; nothing pushed, amended or rebased)

| Stage | SHA | Subject | Files |
|---|---|---|---|
| rp_harden | `d6c8b69` | fix(radio): drop a JOIN whose end rule passes within 0.3 s | `radio_policy.{c,h}` |
| rp_harden | `509e6b6` | fix(radio): pace a repeat Connect's pulse as the router retry (M4) | `radio_policy.{c,h}` |
| WP9 | `ca8ed64` | build(wp9): stage W1 and W2, the Wi-Fi memory lines, off by default | `sdkconfig.wp9/` |
| WP9 | `f3ef9cc` | build(wp9): stage W3 to W6, the NimBLE memory lines, off by default | `sdkconfig.wp9/` |
| WP9 | `1d64367` | build(wp9): stage W7 (mbedTLS) and W8 (cloud_tx stack), off by default | `sdkconfig.wp9/` |
| WP9 | `575e725` | test(wp9): add a bench-only NimBLE pool diagnostic for the G-M gates | `sdkconfig.wp9/` |
| WP9 | `e3cad30` | docs(wp9): explain the staged memory set, its builds and its landing | `sdkconfig.wp9/README.md` |
| M4 fix | `4e8a8cd` | fix(radio): pace a JOIN like a Connect, one stamp for both (M4) | `radio_policy.{c,h}`, `app_wifi.c` (comments) |
| M4 fix | `384affc` | fix(radio): log a Connect that ended before its SUBMIT pulse | `radio_policy.{c,h}`, `app_wifi.c` (comments) |
| WB fix | `1ec70c7` | fix(ble): keep the scan gate's stamp current while BLE is idle | `app_ble_leak.c` |
| WB fix | `dbaae0f` | fix(radio): clear the starvation guard's spacing once it runs out | `radio_policy.c` |
| WB fix | `b651701` | fix(radio): time provisional SERVE from its start, not to an end | `radio_policy.c` |

**`b651701` is phase 3's last firmware commit; CP7 is of it (15t).** Everything after it is docs (this stage's commits).

**M4 and the JOIN, as built** (`main/radio_policy/radio_policy.h` documents each constant)
- **`d6c8b69`.** `ff0b424` already dropped a JOIN once its end rule had passed; a JOIN still pending one or two ticks before that was still granted, a 10-20 ms pulse that restarted I2b's spacing and forced the 1.2 s recovery (the shadow model of `52ef6a2`: 5 such pulses in 100 seeds). The grant now also drops a JOIN whose end rule passes within `RP_PULSE_MIN_MS` (0.3 s), with no line. `sta_settled()` now compares ticks signed: a lease or page stamped by another task after the caller read its tick read as 2^32 ticks old, so the end rule read as passed (used by the grant, the pulse's end and I7's `join_settling()`). Closes LEAK-4's last gap.
- **`509e6b6`, then `4e8a8cd` (the rule as it stands).** The brief's rule (a SUBMIT within 30 s of another takes the 6 s spacing) does not fix M4: Connects 15 s apart already have about 12 s of profile time between pulses, so the pulse still starts when the attacker chooses (leak model: 62.0 / 135.5 s p99.9 at p_loss 0.3 / 0.5 with or without it). As built: **one stamp, `sj_at`, the begin of the last SUBMIT or JOIN pulse. A SUBMIT or JOIN whose pulse would begin less than `RP_REPEAT_MS` (45 s) after it is *paced*:** `RP_RETRY_MS` (1.5 s), granted only at a Coded window's own end, only after I2b's spacing (6 s + U(0, 1) s of profile time), and only when the whole 1.5 s fits the blind budget and I2b's room. A *first* one (no SUBMIT or JOIN pulse begun in 45 s) is unchanged: a SUBMIT is exempt from the spacing and stops BLE within one executor wake for up to 2.8 s; a JOIN needs the spacing, as before, and can be granted into a young Coded scan. The claim-first rule under a leak response, I2, I2b's 12 s per 60 s and the 2.5 s kept for a claim are unchanged; claims never set the stamp. `509e6b6` paced only a repeat Connect; the leak review showed that a fresh MAC per join bought immediate 2.8 s JOIN pulses (LEAK-M4-J: 62.0 / 135.5 s in SERVE-A, 62.5 / 137.0 s in AP_IDLE at `509e6b6`), hence one stamp for both kinds.
- **Why 45 s, not 30 s.** 30 s is a multiple of the 15 s heartbeat: a Connect every 30 s keeps full immediate pulses on every second burst, p99.9 60.5 s (P60 0.9989) at p_loss 0.5, past G2. Going back to 30 s is one line (`RP_REPEAT_MS`) plus its `_Static_assert` (`radio_policy.c:1188`, which also pins the log text).
- **`384affc` (UX-M4-3).** `radio_policy_note_submit(false)` withdraws a pending SUBMIT in the same critical section that clears `s_submit` (no grant race), and prints at most once per 10 s `SUBMIT pulse not granted before its Connect ended (asked N ms before) - the attempt ran beside BLE`. Its rate stamp belongs to the wifi_manager task.
- **The leak model** (the council's physics unchanged, n = 20,000, the attacker's worst timing; scripts and outputs in `…\finish3\m4j\` with `rp_harden\model\` and `rev_leak_m4\`). As built, the worst p99.9 over join floods, Connect floods, mixes, 3 s floods, chained Connects, SERVE-A and AP_IDLE is **30.5 / 47.5 s at p_loss 0.3 / 0.5, P60 ≥ 0.9992** (`509e6b6`: 62.5 / 137.0 s; `52ef6a2`: 62.0 / 135.5 s). **Cite `m4j\out\sp_*`, `c2_sharedsp_*` and `sharedsp_*` for the committed rule**; in `leak_sj.py` the variant named `built` (outputs `built_*`, `c2_built_*`) is the *rejected* no-spacing candidate (the council re-checked the naming and the figures).
- **Rejected (UX-M4-1, UX-M4-2): drop the spacing for a paced Connect.** It reopens M4: a stranger who sends a paced Connect 3-7 s after each timed join can steer the rows' phase so the next join's pulse lands where the burst gets the least Coded scan: p99.9 60.5-61.5 s, P60 0.9965 at p_loss 0.5, past G2 (`m4j\out\c2_built_*`); with the spacing kept the same chains give 46.5 s at most. The UX cost stays, for the user to ratify (15x) and to measure (G-CNA S2).
- **Invariants** (shadow model `m4j\shadow_sj.py`, generated by `mk_shadow_sj.py`): normal sessions, retries, Connect floods, join floods every 15 s and 4 s, mixes: **0 findings** for I1, I2, the recovery, I2b (total, the spacing, each paced pulse ≤ 1.5 s) and I8; I7 unchanged under join floods (14 vs 13 `RETRY during JOIN` in 40 seeds) and 0 in normal sessions. Under a leak response identical to `509e6b6`: 35-39 claims per 900 s, the first at 2.4 s.

**Costs, for the user to ratify** (shadow model, normal sessions; 15x)

| | As built (`4e8a8cd`) | `509e6b6` | `52ef6a2` |
|---|---|---|---|
| SUBMIT grant latency p50 / p90 / p99 | 0.68 / 4.3 / 8.3 s | 0 / 0.75 / 8.8 s | – / 0.36 / 3.2 s |
| Connects with no pulse | 6 of 288 | 0 of 260 | – |
| JOIN pulses of 1.5 s or less | 680 of 1,613 | 59 of 1,635 | – |
| Valve claims outside a leak response | 1,453 | 1,512 | – |

- **A person's first Connect is usually paced:** it typically comes 10-45 s after their own phone's JOIN pulse, so its pulse starts 0.4-1.2 s later and lasts 1.5 s instead of up to 2.8 s. It cannot block setup: the requester never waits, the SUBMIT waits until its Connect ends, and at worst the attempt runs beside BLE (SERVE's Wi-Fi slots), which the new line reports. HANDOFF finding 3 (around line 918) recorded submits that ran with BLE on failing about 1 in 3 times: G-CNA S2 must measure the paced path's success rate and time to GOT_IP.
- **A second phone's join within 45 s of a pulse** gets a paced 1.5 s JOIN; `JOIN pulse not granted (not granted within 10 s)` W lines can be more frequent, because a paced JOIN also waits for the spacing and a Coded window's end.
- **About 4 % fewer valve claims outside a leak response** (a paced JOIN stays asked longer, which holds I7's `join_settling()`). Claims under a leak response skip I7 (`radio_policy_exec_connect_len()`: `!lr && radio_policy_join_settling()`), so RMLEAK/CLOSE delivery is not affected.
- **LR-CLAIM-GAP** (from `509e6b6`, unchanged by `4e8a8cd` and `384affc`): during a leak response with the valve unlinked and a Connect flood, the longest claim-to-claim gap rose from 36-39 s to 44-45 s (a paced SUBMIT takes its own 6-7 s spacing between claims). It stays below the 48.2-48.9 s of the 8-15 s Connect pattern, which predates phase 3. The first claim (2.4 s), claim-first and RMLEAK-before-CLOSE are unchanged. For decisions 6 and 9 of 15u (B1).
- **WAIT-BLOCKS** (accepted): with one stamp a JOIN can never pass a waiting paced SUBMIT, because it would be paced too; the optional change is moot.
- **`WIFI_MANAGER_SCAN_GAP_MS` (20 s) is load-bearing for M4:** a timed list-scan flood every 15 s would give 62-75.5 s at p_loss 0.5 in the model; at the real 20 s and 30 s rates it gives 47.0 s at most. Any reduction of that gap needs the model re-run.
- **G1 (15v):** K1's assists are JOINs, so one within 45 s of a SUBMIT or JOIN pulse is paced too.
- **Small logging gaps (council, accepted):** a quick back-to-back retry can lose its SUBMIT with no line (the old Connect's pulse is ON and ending, the new Connect sets the re-ask flag, then ends before the executor handles the end: the state is ON, not PENDING, so `lost` is false; `radio_policy_exec_pulse_end()` then finds `s_submit` false and drops the request); in BLE_IDLE a Connect that starts and ends within one executor wake can print the not-granted line though no BLE scan runs. Logging only; once per 10 s at most.
- **A stale comment:** `app_ble_leak.c:870-875` still says "SUBMIT is exempt from the spacing only. A SUBMIT or a JOIN stops the running scan at once"; that now holds for a *first* SUBMIT or JOIN only. Suggested text: a first SUBMIT is exempt from the spacing; a first SUBMIT or JOIN stops the scan at once; one within 45 s of the last SUBMIT or JOIN pulse is paced as RETRY (1.5 s from a Coded window's own end, after the spacing). Not a docs file: for the next firmware commit.

**Log lines new in phase 3** (tag `RADIO`; none prints a credential; the production tool's patterns match none)
- The pulse lines gain a suffix when paced: `JOIN pulse for station <MAC>: BLE off for up to 1500 ms (paced: a SUBMIT or JOIN pulse began less than 45 s before)` and `SUBMIT pulse: BLE off for up to 1500 ms (paced: a SUBMIT or JOIN pulse began less than 45 s before)`; their `… pulse over after N ms (…)` lines are unchanged.
- `SUBMIT pulse waits for the pulse spacing (x.x of y.y s): paced, a SUBMIT or JOIN pulse began less than 45 s before`, and `SUBMIT pulse waits for I2b's room: x.x s of pulses in the last 60 s` (`, 2.5 s kept for the valve's claim` under a leak response): one stamp, at most one of the two per 10 s; the claim-first line keeps its own limit.
- `SUBMIT pulse not granted before its Connect ended (asked N ms before) - the attempt ran beside BLE`, at most once per 10 s.
- `509e6b6`'s `SUBMIT pulse waits: a Connect's pulse began less than 45 s ago - …` was replaced by `4e8a8cd`: it is in no CP7 image.

**The whole-branch review** (on `fix/2.1.4` at `d0d2bd3`, with `d6c8b69` and `509e6b6`; the ten findings that survived verification, none from the portal lens)

| ID | Lens | Severity, verdict | In short | Fate |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| WB-CRASH-1 | crash | **major**, confirmed | In BLE_IDLE or PAUSED nothing refreshed the executor's `retry_at`; after 2^31 ticks (248.5 days at 100 Hz) the scan-start gate read it as future and **no BLE scan, valve relink or RMLEAK/CLOSE delivery** happened until a reboot or another 248.5 days | **fixed `1ec70c7`**: the idle block moves a stamp that has come due up to now (a pending settle or retry is kept) |
| WB-CRASH-2 | crash | minor, confirmed | `s_prov_until` (provisional SERVE's end) was never cleared: 248.5 days after the last lease it read as future, so every AP mode ran SERVE (about 50 % BLE) instead of AP_IDLE (about 67 %); the starvation guard's `starve_next` had the same flaw | **fixed `b651701`** (the lease stores its start, `s_prov_at`, read with `rp_within()`) and **`dbaae0f`** (`guard_update()` clears the spacing the pass it comes due) |
| WB-CRASH-3 | crash | minor, confirmed | A backed-off valve silent 248.5 days or more: at its return the stale `s_claim_not_before` reads as future, no claim (and no hunt slot) for up to another 248.5 days unless a leak response is pending | **not changed** (the fixer's sweep: the back-off applies only while `s_claim_fails > 0`, which resets after a link held 60 s or a target change, and a leak response skips it); accepted residual, the same fix pattern after CP7 (15x) |
| WB-CONC-1 | concurrency | minor, confirmed | A claim's CONNECT pulse is sized to exactly the I2 budget left, but its start and end pass through log calls on the shared UART lock (`ble_valve_claim_start()` logs before `ble_gap_connect()`; the CONNECT handler kicks the executor after four banner lines), otherwise the executor learns of the end at its next 500 ms poll: a failed claim's blind span exceeds I2's 2.8 s, and with console contention the 2.9 s monitor | **open** (15x); bench note in 15t's boot "Never" list: an `I2: BLE went N ms` line right after a claim, N up to about 3.4 s |
| LEAK-WB-1 | leak | minor, confirmed | `s_interlock_ok` (the valve confirmed RMLEAK=1 and CLOSED) clears only when the incident latch drops: a direct valve swap during an active incident leaves it set from the old valve, so the new, open valve gets no leak-response treatment (the LR overlay, the back-off exemption) until a source sends another wet report. The rules engine still closes the new valve at its first link while a sensor is wet (CHANGELOG *Safety*) | **open** (15x); bench: T3-06's swap during a live incident |
| LEAK-WB-2 | leak | minor, confirmed | On a hub with no BLE leak sensor (valve alone, or valve and LoRa) BLE_IDLE time while the valve is linked is not I2b profile time, so after a later drop the first relink claim waits 6-7 s of scanning, also a claim carrying a pended RMLEAK/CLOSE (I2b protects BLE sensor coverage, which this hub does not have) | **open** (15x); a 6-7 s delay of a pended CLOSE on such hubs |
| WB-CLOUD-1 | cloud | minor, confirmed | `C2D_COMMANDS.md` §3.5 (lines 155, 158, 161) promises the cloud team that buffered events precede a reconnect's lifecycle, a cmd_ack precedes the snapshot and the twin report its command causes; 15q's "The wire order cmd_ack, twin, snapshot is kept" repeats it. The cmd_ack is published directly on the esp-mqtt task in the C2D handler (not drain-first, not held behind the lifecycle), and one the outbox refuses (−2) is kept for the 10 s replay while the command's twin and snapshot go out at once | **open** (15x): either correct the contract (`C2D_COMMANDS.md` is outside `docs/`, so not edited here) or route the cmd_ack through `cloud_tx`. **15q's sentence is corrected by this note:** the order holds only while MQTT accepts the cmd_ack. The CHANGELOG's release-candidate section and `V5_DELTA_FOR_APP_DOC.md` say so |
