**The stale-stamp fixes** (`…\finish3\fix_wbcrash\`): each compiles under the production and lab headers with no warning and no IRAM, DRAM or RTC section; per-symbol diffs show only the intended functions changed; behaviour is the same below 2^31 ticks. `1ec70c7`: legitimately `retry_at` is never more than one slot plus `SCAN_SETTLE_MS` ahead and idle passes run at least every 500 ms (`want_row` NONE), and in that pass neither the start gate nor the wait reads the stamp. `dbaae0f`: `guard_update()` runs on every executor pass (`exec_mode` at `app_ble_leak.c:1472`); the start test becomes `starve_next == 0`; executor-only state, no lock; the guard never starts in LR_AP. `b651701`: `s_prov_at` is `rp_nz()`'d (never 0), written under `s_sta_lock`, read lock-free as an aligned word; the one change is a lease stamped after the executor read its tick counts as not started for that one pass, and the lease's kick runs the next pass at once; LR_AP outranks SERVE. **The sweep** found no other stamp of the kind: `w_end_at`, the claim and pulse deadlines, `starve_until`, station `join_at`, `app_ble_valve.c`'s claim back-off (WB-CRASH-3 above), `wifi_manager.c`'s `stale_leave_until`, the rules engine (epoch seconds), and the I2 monitor after an idle stretch (`coded_budget()` is 0 with a stale `coded_end_at`). **Deferred (after CP7):** `ticks_ms()` in `app_ble_leak.c` (`(uint32_t)t * portTICK_PERIOD_MS`) wraps once a span passes 49.7 days, so a `coded_end_at` that stale can read as a few hundred ms old (`coded_budget()`, the pulse-end blind/recovery test, `row_begin()`'s lead, the I2 monitor): on leaving such an idle stretch the wrapped value falls under the 2.8 s budget with probability about 6.5e-7, and the worst result is one extra blind span of 2.8 s or less or one missed RECOVERY row; nothing on the leak, auto-close or RMLEAK/CLOSE path depends on it. The fix is to saturate at `INT32_MAX` ms, **not** `UINT32_MAX` (`row_begin()`, about `app_ble_leak.c:1273`, adds `RP_I8_WIFI_MIN_MS + RP_JITTER_MS` to the result and would overflow), then re-check all six callers; `radio_policy.c`'s `rp_ms()` has the same shape but feeds only log figures and capped spans. `d6c8b69`'s signed `sta_settled()` could misread a lease older than 2^31 ticks, but only a station asked for a JOIN within the last 10 s reaches it: unreachable.

**WP9: the memory set, staged and off by default** (plan §8, §11, D14; `sdkconfig.wp9/`; the bench procedure, with the review's corrections, is **`docs/field_logs/2.1.4/WP9_GM_PROCEDURE.md`**). One defaults fragment per plan row, two staged patches, one bench-only diagnostic and a README. **No build reads the folder unless its command names it; no source, `sdkconfig.defaults` or Kconfig file changed; CP7's image is unchanged.** kconfgen 2.5.0 with the build's own `config.env`, `Kconfig` and renames reproduces CP7's `sdkconfig` and `sdkconfig.h` byte for byte and gives each variant's hash (the reviewer reproduced all eight); every symbol exists in ESP-IDF 5.5.1 with its range and prerequisites; all 32 project sources compile against each variant header with only the four known warnings, and the IDF sources the lines touch (81 NimBLE, 24 mbedTLS/esp-tls, `wifi_init.c`) with none; all three patches pass `git apply --check` at HEAD.

| Line | What | Frees at rest (corrected) | State after the review |
|---|---|---|---|
| W1 | `ESP_WIFI_IRAM_OPT`, `ESP_WIFI_RX_IRAM_OPT` off | **17,580 B** of IRAM code to flash (CP5's map: 7,095 + 99 + 81 + 3,364 + 6,941 B); DIRAM `.text` 113,387 → about 95,800 B; fallback (IRAM_OPT alone) 7,275 B | bench |
| W2 | `ESP_WIFI_STATIC_RX_BUFFER_NUM` 10 → 6 | about 6.4 KB | bench |
| W3 | `BT_NIMBLE_TRANSPORT_EVT_COUNT` 30 → 12 | 4,968 B; its failure is a reboot (`host_rcv_pkt`'s assert) | bench, after the pool diagnostic |
| W4 | ACL 24 → 8, `MSYS_2` 24 → 12 | **8,704 B**; a shortage can block the controller's VHCI receive up to 29 ticks (about 290 ms at 100 Hz) and silently drop a valve notification (WP9-ADV-4) | bench, after the pool diagnostic |
| W5 | `BT_NIMBLE_MAX_CONNECTIONS` 3 → 1 | 528 B | small for its risk (`rc=6` claims) |
| **W6** | `ROLE_PERIPHERAL`, `ROLE_BROADCASTER` off | 1,226 B + tables | **Never bench, never ship (WP9-ADV-1, critical).** In IDF 5.5.1 `BLE_GATTS` = 0 compiles out NimBLE's Handle Value Notification and Indication receipt (`ble_att.c` 62-80 dispatch only under `MYNEWT_VAL(BLE_GATTS)`; `ble_att_svr.c` whole-file) and the ATT error response: the hub would lose every valve notification (the flood probe's wet report, RMLEAK 1→0 from a long press, a manual open or close, a battery at 10 % or less) while its gate (VAL-01, G6b, P11, P14: CCCD writes and read-backs) still passes. Its fragment, README and patch header say the GATT client stays and an ATT request gets an error: both false |
| W7 | `MBEDTLS_DYNAMIC_FREE_CONFIG_DATA` | 0 B on this hub | do not ship (recommended) |
| W8 | `cloud_tx` stack 5,120 → 4,096 B (a source constant: a patch) | 1,024 B `.bss` | only if T6-11 shows ≥ 1,536 B free at 5,120 B |

The plan's IRAM figure (">27 KB") is 17.6 KB on this image and W4's 7.8 KB is 8.7 KB: plan §8 and D14 carry a dated note now.

### 15x. Decisions for the user: every open item of 15q-15w in one list (2026-10-07)

Recommendations are the fixers', reviewers' and councils'; the source section is in brackets. Items 1-7 are choices the orchestrator made under the instruction of 2026-10-06 and the user has not seen.

**A. Ratify what was built overnight**
1. **B1:** claims keep their back-off exemption while a leak response is pending. *Recommended: ratify* (15u; 15s item 1).
2. **B2:** the valve hunt slot in NORMAL (N_HUNT, 0.45 s on 1M) and a back-off only after two empty claims; **restate the 100-power-cycle gate statistically** (for example at most 2 of 100 over 10 s, with the valve's adverts per 1M slot recorded; the model gives P(all 100 ≤ 10 s) = 0.93 / 0.18 / 0.00 at valve p_loss 0.1 / 0.3 / 0.5), and G6's "linked ≤ 5 s" likewise; the bench picks 0.3 or 0.45 s. *Recommended* (15u decision 2; 15s item 2).
3. **B3:** a 1M share while any listed sensor's PHY is unknown, at most 10 min after boot or provisioning; **M5** (a 1M sensor first powered more than 10 min later gets only N_CODED's 20 % until first heard): optional code after G0. *Recommended: ratify; M5 after G0* (15u decisions 1, 8; 15s item 3).
4. **B4 and MB-3:** NimBLE's log cap in `sdkconfig`; keep `CONFIG_BT_NIMBLE_PRINT_ERR_NAME=y` (changing it moves the CP7 hash). *Recommended* (15u decision 12).
5. **B5:** a link that reached CONNECT is not a failed claim. *Recommended: ratify* (15u; 15s item 5).
6. **The SUBMIT rule and M4's pacing, with their costs:** a first Connect always honoured and exempt from I2b's spacing but counted in I2b and I2; a SUBMIT or JOIN within 45 s of the last SUBMIT or JOIN pulse paced (1.5 s, at a Coded window's end, after the spacing); 45 s, not 30 s; the spacing kept for paced Connects (UX-M4-1's no-spacing variant rejected at 61.5 s). Cost: a person's Connect within 45 s of their own phone's join is paced (0.4-1.2 s later, 1.5 s instead of up to 2.8 s; model p90 grant latency 4.3 s; 6 of 288 Connects with no pulse); a repeat Connect under a leak response with a heard-but-unlinked valve mostly yields to the claims. *Recommended: ratify, then judge on G-CNA S2's success rate and time to GOT_IP* (15u decision 5; 15w).
7. **The NORMAL claim length,** 1.5-2.5 s (the plan says 1.5 s). *Recommended: keep for CP7, decide on the bench numbers* (15u decision 3).
8. **I10's heap exception,** about 30 B for the `ble_phy` namespace entry. *Recommended: approve* (15s item 4).

**B. Decide on the bench numbers**
9. **PH-2, the tail:** keep "TAIL runs NORMAL" with P-6, P-8 and P-9 blocking in S2; if they fail, the bounded tail rule (about 10 lines) or the 2-line option (defer non-LR claims while the SoftAP has a station). *Decide after S2* (15u decision 4).
10. **PH-7:** "Other Network" after a success can start a switch late in the 60 s tail. *Keep for CP7; if users hit it, show the remaining time or relabel the button "Back"* (15r decision 1).
11. **The list-scan pulses:** exempt the first LIST after a station's lease from the spacing (as a first SUBMIT is), and let the gate wait out the requesting phone's own assist tail (≤ 1.5 s) instead of refusing. *Decide with G1's list-completeness data* (15u decision 7). Keep `WIFI_MANAGER_SCAN_GAP_MS` at 20 s or more unless the M4 model is re-run (15w).
12. **D4's leak-path reordering:** only if CP7 measures a leak → CLOSE over 200 ms (15q item 2).
13. **The G-M lines (WP9), each:** the "pays" threshold (the median `idma: free` at rest rises by at least half the estimate: *confirm*); **W7: drop** (*recommended*); W5 small for its risk; W8 waits for T6-11; **W6 never** (above); then each line's landing (WP9_GM_PROCEDURE.md section 7) (15w).

**C. Design choices still open**
14. **The QoS 0 twin report** with IoT Hub's 204 as its acknowledgement (15p W1): not built without a decision (15q item 1).
15. **`network.timeout_ms`** (15m): no decision yet (15q item 3).
16. **15i residual 6:** `esp_mqtt_client_start()`'s result is still unchecked (15q item 4).
17. **A claim delayed by pulses made before a leak response:** reserve the claim's room whenever the valve is wanted and unlinked, or exempt an episode's first claim from the room check (worst case about 60 s, RMLEAK/CLOSE delayed, never lost) (15u decision 6).
18. **LEAK-6's option:** defer only LIST (not RETRY) in LR_AP's first 30 s (15u decision 11).
19. **Accept** the B1 interplay (claims use nearly all of I2b's room with a heard-but-unlinked valve), a dead valve hunted at the full rate while the incident is latched, and LEAK-3 (a known-1M sensor can be 1M-blind about 5.2 s around a pulse) (15u decisions 8-10).
20. **The open setup SoftAP** (WPA2 excluded by the user): M4 is closed in the model; joins with new MACs still defer non-LR claims and list scans 10 s each (I7), and a stranger can make the hub forget its Wi-Fi (D9 kept). A per-client Connect limit is the remaining option (15r decision 3; 15u residual 5).
21. **The whole-branch review's open findings,** each a small firmware change after CP7: **WB-CONC-1** (size a claim's pulse with a margin for the logging, or kick the executor before the banners), **LEAK-WB-1** (clear `s_interlock_ok` on a valve target change), **LEAK-WB-2** (count BLE_IDLE time with a linked valve as profile time, or exempt a pended RMLEAK/CLOSE claim from the spacing on a hub with no BLE sensor), **WB-CRASH-3** and the **49.7-day `ticks_ms()` wrap** (saturate at `INT32_MAX` ms) (15w).
22. **WB-CLOUD-1:** correct `C2D_COMMANDS.md` §3.5 (the cmd_ack order holds only while MQTT accepts the ack; a refused ack is replayed within 10 s, after its twin and snapshot) **or** send the cmd_ack through `cloud_tx`. *The docs owner recommends correcting the contract for 2.1.4 and the code later* (15w).

**D. Release steps (not for CP7)**

**E. Before any push** (one history rewrite, already planned)
29. **Redact `6b84ae3`'s Wi-Fi password** (`docs/field_logs/2.1.3/UART logs.txt` lines 137/147; plan §9). `origin/master` is still `ae4d59a`.

If every subject on the branch must be 72 characters or less, 43 are longer (most from 2026-09-25/26): `git log --format='%h %s' ae4d59a..HEAD | Where-Object { $_.Length -gt 80 }` lists them.
